# Plan 13 — Per-Academy Feature Toggles — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Etqan switches whole parts of the product on or off for each academy from the platform admin. A switched-off feature disappears from that academy's API, dashboard and background jobs; switching it back on restores everything. The academy's admin sees the switches read-only.

**Architecture:**
- **The registry.** `etqan.platform.features` defines the 36 features once in code: 8 built and 28 not yet, each with labels, a group, a default, `built` and `requires`.
  - `features.enabled(code)` reads the academy that django-tenants has already put on the connection (the request's, or `for_each_academy`'s).
  - So it costs no query, and the platform never imports the tenants app.
- **The values.** `Academy.features` (public schema, `db_default={}`) maps a code to a boolean; a missing code means the default.
  - A data migration carries each academy's Plan 12b "General supervision" into `features["supervision"]`.
  - The old column stays in place and is no longer written.
- **Enforcement.**
  - Routes declare `feature` next to `permission_codes`. `FeatureOn` runs after the code check and answers 404, and the route table gains its feature column.
  - Every other effect sits in the service that owns it: the payer rule, sign-in, `me/`, the student payload, automatic invoices, teacher attendance, CSV export and the notifications scanner.
- **Reading them.** `me/` lists the features that are on. `GET academy/features/` lists them all for the academy's admin.
- **Etqan's side.**
  - The Academy change page in the platform admin gains a Features section, the "Copy features from another academy" action, and history.
  - `manage.py set_features` does the same from the command line; the e2e suite uses it.
- **The dashboard.**
  - `hasFeature()` sits next to `can()`. Nav items, route `staticData` and action buttons name their feature.
  - A switched-off screen opened directly says "This feature isn't enabled for your academy."
  - Settings → Features is new and read-only, and the General supervision switch becomes read-only.

**Tech Stack:** Django 6 / DRF 3.18 / django-tenants 3.14, PostgreSQL 18; React 19 + TanStack Router/Query + Vite (base `/app/`), react-hook-form + zod 4, i18next, Radix UI; Playwright through the Caddy edge.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-feature-toggles-design.md` (Phase B1, plan 4 of 4). This plan builds on:
- Plan 1: the `Academy` tenant and the platform admin;
- Plans 3–12b: every switchable feature, and the route table in `access/tests/test_routes.py`;
- Plan 8: the scanner;
- Plan 12b: `supervision_enabled`, the first switch, which this plan absorbs.

Where this plan fills a gap in the spec, the Decisions below say so.

**Verified:** a script applied the code in Tasks 1–11 in this plan's order, exactly as written, to fresh copies of `backend@64777bb` and `dashboard@b8a2fdd` (the current `main` of each) and to a copy of meta's `STATE.md`. That covers every `Create` and `replace … with` block, the `makemigrations` command and the route-tree regeneration.
- After each task, the resulting tree was identical to the reference branch the plan was written from. Only the generated migration's `# Generated by Django … on <date>` line differs, and the `STATE.md` edits produce the intended file.
- Before each task's code went in, its new tests were run and failed; the ones that passed are guards named in their task's Step 2.
- After each task, its format, lint, import-contract (18 kept throughout), type, test and coverage commands passed. `ruff format --check` and `biome check` found the plan's code already formatted.
- Backend: 2016 → 2219 tests. Per task: 2028, 2030, 2174, 2194, 2198, 2207, 2219. Coverage 98.11% → 98.27%.
- Dashboard: 880 → 906 tests (894, 902, 906). Lines 94.56% → 94.58%, branches 89.48% → 89.68%, functions 83.13% → 82.99%.

The e2e suite then passed through Caddy, run the way the CI `e2e` job runs it:
- Django (`config.settings.local`, the file email backend, eager Celery) and the Vite preview, on a freshly migrated and seeded scratch database;
- the marketing server on `:4321`;
- a `caddy:2.11.4-alpine` edge on `:80` in front of Django on `:8000`.

That was 23 of 23, `features.spec.ts` and the updated `supervision.spec.ts` included, twice on the same database with `--retries=0` and one worker.

## Global Constraints

**Repos and branches**
- Repos: `backend/` (trunk `main`), `dashboard/` (trunk `main`), meta repo (trunk `master`).
- The meta repo is already on `feat/features`, and the spec is committed there.
- Before Task 1 and Task 8, the controller creates `feat/features` in `backend/` (from `main` at `64777bb`) and in `dashboard/` (from `main` at `b8a2fdd`). Check with `git -C backend branch --show-current` and `git -C dashboard branch --show-current`.
- Nothing is merged without the user's approval. After merge, bump the submodule pointers in meta.
- Commit messages: Conventional Commits, ending with exactly `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

**Backend commands**
- Run from `backend/` with the virtualenv `backend/.venv`. Export this environment once per shell:
  `export DATABASE_URL=postgres://etqan:etqan@localhost:55432/etqan CELERY_BROKER_URL=redis://localhost:56379/0 DJANGO_SECRET_KEY=test-secret DJANGO_READ_DOT_ENV_FILE=False DJANGO_TENANT_BASE_DOMAIN=etqan.localhost`
- Tests: `.venv/bin/pytest …`. Add `--create-db` once after Tasks 1 and 2, which add migrations.
- Format: `.venv/bin/ruff check --fix . && .venv/bin/ruff format .`
- Verify: `.venv/bin/ruff check . && .venv/bin/ruff format --check . && .venv/bin/lint-imports && .venv/bin/pytest -q --cov=etqan`
- Ruff selects `PL`, `FBT`, `DTZ`, `BLE`, `ERA`, `C901` (complexity 10) and `E501` (88 columns). Migrations are excluded from Ruff.

**Dashboard commands**
- Run from `dashboard/` through `npx pnpm@10`.
- Format: `npx pnpm@10 exec biome check --write src e2e` (it also sorts imports).
- A new route file is picked up by the TanStack Router plugin. In Task 10, regenerate `src/routeTree.gen.ts` with `npx pnpm@10 exec vite build` before `tsc`, and commit it.
- Verify: `npx pnpm@10 exec vite build && npx pnpm@10 tsc --noEmit && npx pnpm@10 lint && npx pnpm@10 test:coverage && npx pnpm@10 build`
- `pnpm lint` is `biome ci . && node scripts/check-colors.mjs`: no hex colour literals and no Tailwind palette utilities, comments included. Semantic tokens only.
  - `biome ci` reports 5 warnings that are already on `main`. They don't fail it, and this plan adds none.

**Coverage and dev data**
- Coverage gates: backend ≥ 80% (`pytest --cov=etqan`); dashboard lines 80 / statements 80 / branches 70 / functions 70.
- Seeded dev password `e2e-EtqanTest-2026`, admins only.
  - `demo` (`admin@demo.test`) has the "Omar family" (Yusuf Omar and Aisha Omar) and Sara Supervisor.
  - `other` is `admin@other.test`.

**Tenancy and module boundaries**
- `Academy.features` lives on the tenant, in the public schema (`SHARED_APPS`). No tenant-schema column is added. `migrate_schemas` runs the public schema first, then every academy.
- **Migrations are blue/green safe** (STATE.md's standing warning).
  - `features` has `db_default={}`, so the old colour keeps inserting academies.
  - `AcademySettings.supervision_enabled` stays in place, unwritten; a later release drops it.
- **`etqan.platform` imports no business module** (its lint-imports contract). `features.enabled()` reads `connection.tenant.features` and never imports `etqan.tenants`.
- Apps read their switches through `etqan.platform.features`. Only `etqan.tenants` writes them: `services.set_features`, `copy_features` and `seed_features`, and its admin.
- No new lint-imports contract is needed: 18 contracts stay kept.
- **No positional boolean flags:** `set_features(academy, values)` takes a dict, `copy_features(source, targets)` takes academies, and the test fixture takes keyword switches (`set_features(families=False)`).

**API and data rules** (spec values verbatim)
- The registry has 36 entries (§3).
  - A feature is on when "its stored value (or, with none stored, its default) is on **and** every feature in `requires` is on".
  - "An unknown code in storage is ignored."
- `enabled(code)`: "An unknown code is a programming error and raises" (`LookupError`).
- A switched-off feature's routes answer **404** after the permission check, with the body `{"detail": "This feature is not enabled for this academy."}`.
- Parent sign-in while parents are off: `403 {"detail": "Parent access is not enabled for this academy."}`.
- A teacher's attendance while teacher attendance is off: `403 {"detail": "Teacher attendance is not enabled for this academy."}`.
- `me/` gains `features`: "the codes that are effectively on, in registry order".
- `GET /api/v1/academy/features/` is for admins only (no staff code, as `people/admins/`). It returns "every feature with `code, label_ar, label_en, group, on, built, held_by`".
- "Nothing in the tenant API writes `Academy.features`": the new route is GET only, with no POST, PUT, PATCH or DELETE.
- **Junk never 500s:**
  - a non-boolean stored value reads as the default;
  - the admin's copy action refuses a junk source (Django's action bar validates the choice);
  - `set_features` names an unknown code.
- **Query counts:** `enabled()` adds no query anywhere. `me/` costs the same whatever the academy's switches.
- **Other academies:** a switch in one academy never reaches another's requests or scan. The scan test forces the other academy's pks above this one's (`until_pk_exceeds`).

**Dashboard strings and helpers**
- Every string is in `src/locales/en/common.json` and `src/locales/ar/common.json`.
- Match accessible names exactly where one is a prefix of another: "Family accounts", "Families", "Family", "Family to add to", "Account type".
- Reuse `can`/`useCan`, `NoAccess`'s layout, `StatusChip`, `PageHeader` and `EmptyState`; make no copies. The seven "Export CSV" links become one `ExportButton`.
- Screens work RTL and at phone width. Settings → Features is one card per group.

**Tests**
- Each new assertion fails without the code under test. Guards that pass beforehand are named in their task's Step 2.
- Tests switch features with the root `set_features` fixture (Task 1).
  - It writes the academy row and the object on the connection.
  - It gives the session-shared academies their switches back afterwards.
- Query counts leave out django-tenants' `SET search_path` statements.

### Decisions this plan makes where the spec is silent or leaves a choice

- **D1 — The registry.**
  - **Where it lives.** `etqan.platform.features` (spec §3), a tuple of frozen `Feature(code, label_en, label_ar, group, default, built, requires)`.
    - The platform may not import `etqan.tenants`. `stored()` therefore reads `connection.tenant.features`: the academy django-tenants loaded for the request (middleware) or for the job (`tenant_context` in `for_each_academy`).
    - Outside an academy (the public schema) nothing is stored, so every feature reads its default.
    - An unknown code raises `LookupError("Unknown feature: 'x'")`.
  - **Order.** The 8 built features come first, in the spec's §3.1 order, then the 28 others in §3.2's order. That order is `me/`'s and the API's.
  - **Groups:** `people · teaching · money · communication · content · platform`.
  - **The 36 codes** (built ones marked ●; every default is off except the built ones other than `supervision`):

    | Code | English | Arabic | Group |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | ● `families` | Family accounts | نظام حسابات العائلات | people |
    | ● `parents` | Parents and guardians | نظام أولياء الأمور | people |
    | ● `supervision` (default **off**) | General supervision | نظام الإشراف العام | teaching |
    | ● `session_reports` | Session reports | نظام تقارير الحصص | teaching |
    | ● `invoices` | Invoices and receipts | نظام الفواتير والإيصالات | money |
    | ● `auto_notifications` | Automatic notifications | نظام الإشعارات التلقائية | communication |
    | ● `teacher_attendance` | Teachers record attendance | تسجيل المعلم للحضور | teaching |
    | ● `export` | Excel export (CSV) | نظام تصدير إكسل | platform |
    | `levels` | Levels | نظام المستويات | teaching |
    | `file_uploads` | File uploads | نظام رفع الملفات | content |
    | `url_redirects` | URL redirects | نظام تحويل الروابط | platform |
    | `ai_assistant` | AI assistant | المساعد الذكي | platform |
    | `payment_links` | Payment links | نظام روابط الدفع | money |
    | `verified_certificates` | Verified certificates | نظام الشهادات الموثقة | teaching |
    | `incentives_deductions` | Incentives and deductions | نظام الحوافز والخصومات | money |
    | `free_sessions` | Free sessions for subscribers | نظام الحصص المجانية للمشتركين | money |
    | `homework` | Homework | نظام الواجبات | teaching |
    | `internal_chat` | Internal chat | نظام المحادثات الداخلية | communication |
    | `recorded_courses` | Recorded courses | نظام الدورات المسجلة | content |
    | `articles` | Articles | نظام المقالات | content |
    | `country_pricing` | Per-country pricing | نظام التسعير حسب الدولة | money |
    | `consultations` | Consultations | نظام الاستشارات | teaching |
    | `study_groups` | Study groups | نظام المجموعات الدراسية | teaching |
    | `balances` | Balances | نظام الأرصدة | money |
    | `student_teacher_rate` | Per-student teacher rate | سعر المعلم لكل طالب | money |
    | `donations` | Donations | نظام التبرعات | money |
    | `ai_reports` | AI reports | تقارير الذكاء الاصطناعي | teaching |
    | `contracts` | Contracts | نظام العقود | people |
    | `subscription_archive` | Subscription archive | أرشيف الاشتراكات | teaching |
    | `session_archive` | Session archive | أرشيف الحصص | teaching |
    | `report_deductions` (`requires: session_reports`) | Report deductions | خصومات التقارير | money |
    | `fixed_teacher_salary` | Fixed teacher salary | الراتب الثابت للمعلم | money |
    | `registration_waitlist` | Student registration waiting list | قائمة انتظار تسجيل الطلاب | people |
    | `payment_receipts` | Payment receipt records | سجلات إيصالات الدفع | money |
    | `zoom_api` | Zoom API | ربط Zoom API | platform |
    | `public_links` | Public links | نظام الروابط العامة | platform |

  - **Junk stored values.** A stored value that is not a boolean counts as "not stored", so the default applies.
  - **`held_by`.** It lists the prerequisites holding a feature off, but only when the feature's own switch is on (§5.5: "a checked feature held off by a prerequisite shows why"). Otherwise it is `[]`.
- **D2 — Storage and moving General supervision into the switchboard.**
  - **`Academy.features`.** A `JSONField(default=dict, db_default={}, blank=True)`, added by `tenants 0003` (generated).
  - **`tenants 0004_supervision_into_features`** is a `RunPython` in the **public** schema. It runs before any academy's own migrations.
    - For each academy with no `supervision` key yet, it reads `supervision_enabled` from `"<schema>".academy_academysettings` by raw SQL, and stores `supervision: true` where it was on.
    - An academy whose settings table lacks the column (Plan 12b migrating in the same deploy, so it was off) is skipped. So is one with no settings row.
    - Nothing else is written, so every other academy keeps today's behaviour through the defaults.
  - **The switch itself.**
    - `scheduling.services.supervision_enabled()` is removed. Its callers (`payloads.shows_supervision`, `views._supervisor_field`, the session PATCH and the supervision views) read `features.enabled("supervision")`. In Task 3 the route-level ones become `feature` declarations.
    - `academy/settings/` keeps `supervision_enabled` as a read-only field showing the feature. A PATCH that sends it is ignored, junk included: a 200, not a 400. `update_settings` loses the argument.
    - The column stays in place and is no longer written. Dropping it is a follow-up for a later release (blue/green).
- **D3 — Declaring a route's feature.**
  - A view declares `feature = "<code>"`, a per-method map (`{"PATCH": "supervision"}` on `sessions/<id>/`), or a property: `people/<kind>/` belongs to `parents` only for `kind == "parents"`.
  - `platform.permissions.FeatureOn` goes last in `permission_classes` (`[HasCode, FeatureOn]`, `[READERS, FeatureOn]`). A caller the role or code check refuses keeps its 403 (or 401/403 when signed out). Everyone else gets `404 {"detail": "This feature is not enabled for this academy."}`.
  - **The feature column (spec gap: its shape).** It is a `FEATURES` mapping in `test_routes.py`, keyed by the same `(method, path)` as `ROUTES`, rather than a fourth element in 150 tuples. It covers exactly `ROUTES` + `ADMIN_ONLY`. The test fails:
    - when a view's declared feature differs from the table, including when a feature route is left undeclared;
    - when a feature route does not list `FeatureOn`;
    - when a code is not a built registry feature;
    - when a path naming `/families/`, `/parents/`, `/guardians/`, `/supervision/`, `/report` or `/billing/` is missing from the table.
  - **The mapping:**
    - `families`: `people/families/` (GET, POST), `families/<id>/` (GET, PATCH), `families/payers/`.
    - `parents`: `people/parents/…` (list, create, detail, PATCH, invite, children), and `students/<id>/guardians/` (GET, POST) with its DELETE.
    - `supervision`: `supervision/…` (4 routes) and `sessions/<id>/` PATCH.
    - `session_reports`: `sessions/<id>/report/` (GET, PUT) and `reports/missing/`.
    - `invoices`: all ten `billing/…` routes, `summary/` and `payers/` included.
  - **Not route-level:**
    - The students bulk family actions (`create_family`, `add_to_family`, `remove_from_family`) answer the same 404 inside `StudentBulkView`, since the route's other actions stay.
    - Teacher attendance: D4.
    - `?format=csv` answers 404 because `CSVExportMixin` offers no CSV renderer while export is off, and DRF 404s an unknown `format`.
- **D4 — The effects of each built feature when off (§5.3), and the gaps settled.**
  - **`families`:**
    - the student profile payload (lists, detail, `me/`, children) drops `account_type` and `family`;
    - the students list ignores `?family=` and `?account_type=`, and its CSV drops "Account type" and "Family";
    - the payer rule skips the family payer.
  - **`parents`:**
    - the payer rule skips the guardians;
    - login refuses a `role == "parent"` account with the 403 above (checked right after the credentials, before email verification);
    - `me/` omits `children`, for anyone holding a parent profile.
    - *Gap:* a parent **already signed in** is signed out on the next API request. `etqan.identity.authentication.SessionAuthentication` wraps DRF's, logs the session out and treats the caller as anonymous, so every route then answers 403 and the dashboard goes to sign-in. Anonymous rather than refused, so the CSRF and sign-in routes still work for that browser.
    - *Gap:* the scanner tells no guardian (recipients drop the `GUARDIANS` audience); students, teachers, admins and payers are still told.
    - *Literal reading kept:* with families on and parents off, a family whose payer is a parent keeps that payer.
  - **`session_reports`:** the scanner reads no `report.missing`.
  - **`invoices`:**
    - `invoice_subscription` returns None first, whatever `auto_invoice_on_subscription` says;
    - the scanner reads no `invoice.issued` or `invoice.overdue`;
    - *gap:* subscription rows and their CSV carry no `payment_status` / `payment_overdue` (billing's read is skipped). The dashboard drops the Payment column.
  - **`auto_notifications`:** `scan_now()` returns 0 before reading anything, the pending-email re-queue included. Notices already sent stay in the bell. Settings' `enabled` switches per type still apply when it is on.
  - **`teacher_attendance`:** `scheduling.services.mark_attendance` refuses `by` with `role == "teacher"` (403 with the message above) before anything else. Office users are unaffected.
  - **`export`:** D3.
  - **`supervision`:** unchanged from Plan 12b, only now keyed on the feature.
- **D5 — `me/` and the admin's read.**
  - `me/` gains `features` (`features.enabled_codes()`), for every role, with no extra query.
  - `GET academy/features/` lives in `etqan.academy` (`AcademyFeaturesView`, `permission_codes = {}`). It returns `features.describe()`. The route table lists it under `ADMIN_ONLY`.
- **D6 — The platform admin (bare base domain).**
  - **The change page.** It uses `AcademyChangeForm`, built with `type()` so each of the 36 `feature_<code>` checkboxes is a class-level field Django admin accepts in fieldsets.
    - The fieldsets are "Features: People" … "Features: Platform", in registry order. The first carries the note "Only Etqan switches these…".
    - Labels read "Family accounts (نظام حسابات العائلات)".
    - An unbuilt feature's help text is "Takes effect when built."; a held-off one adds "Held off: needs Session reports.".
    - A box shows the feature's own switch (stored or default), not its effective value.
  - **Saving.** Only the boxes Etqan changed are written into `features`; the rest keep following their defaults. History comes free: Django admin's change message lists the changed fields by label.
  - **"Copy features from another academy"** is an admin action with an `ActionForm` select (`copy_from`). It copies the source's stored map exactly onto each selected academy except the source, and logs "Copied features from <name>." per academy.
    - A missing source is an error message.
    - Junk (`x`, `0`, `10**30`) never reaches the action: Django's action bar rejects it ("No action selected.").
  - **`manage.py set_features <subdomain> --on … --off …`** is the same switch for scripts and the e2e suite. It refuses a missing subdomain, an empty call, a code named both ways, and an unknown code.
  - **No tenant API writes the switches** (§5.5). A test PATCHes `academy/settings/` with `features` and `supervision_enabled` and finds nothing changed, and every write method on `academy/features/` is a 405.
- **D7 — The dashboard.**
  - **`hasFeature` and `useHasFeature`.**
    - `hasFeature(me, code)` and `useHasFeature()` sit in `identity/permissions.tsx`. `CanProvider` (the app shell's) provides both, for every role.
    - `Me.features?` is optional so older fixtures need not list it. A missing list hides nothing; the server always sends it.
    - `FeatureCode` is the 8 built codes.
  - **Nav.** Items name `feature` for: Missing reports and Reports to write (`session_reports`), Invoices, office and family (`invoices`), My supervision, Parents and Families.
    - `visibleNavItems(…, hasFeature)` hides them. The Home tiles use the same filter, and the revenue card also needs `invoices`.
    - `AppShell` no longer reads the academy settings: 12b's `useAcademySettings({ enabled: supervisor })` goes.
  - **Screens.** Route `staticData.feature` names the feature on the screens of families, parents, the two report screens, the five invoice screens and supervision.
    - `PermissionGate` checks the code first, then the feature, and renders `FeatureOff` ("This feature isn't enabled for your academy.", distinct from `NoAccess`).
    - `routes/permissions.test.ts` pins the list and fails on an app-shell screen whose id names one of those features but declares none.
  - **Buttons and sections:**
    - `ExportButton` replaces the seven "Export CSV" links;
    - the students list drops the Account type column, both family filters and the three bulk family actions;
    - the student page drops the family facts and the Guardians panel;
    - the subscription page drops its Invoices panel, and the subscriptions list its Payment column;
    - the session page drops its Report card;
    - a teacher's session table drops the Attendance column (`teacher_attendance`) and the Report column (`session_reports`).
  - **Settings → Features** is `/settings/features`, admins only (`ADMINS_ONLY`, like the route) and in the Settings nav group. It is read-only: one card per group, each feature On/Off, "Takes effect when built" and "Held off: needs …", under a "Managed by Etqan" chip.
  - **The academy form's General supervision checkbox** is disabled, shows the feature, reads "Managed by Etqan: see Settings → Features.", and is never sent.
  - Screens only a parent account sees need nothing more: with parents off the account cannot sign in.
- **D8 — Seeds.**
  - `seed_dev.FEATURES = {"demo": features.BUILT}`: demo gets every built feature on, supervision included. `other` gets nothing stored, so the defaults (supervision off).
  - `tenants.services.seed_features(academy, codes)` switches on only the codes with nothing stored, so a re-run never overrides a switch Etqan changed. It runs for every academy `seed_academy` seeds (`seed_staging` too).
  - `seed_supervision` still puts Sara on Yusuf's Tajweed subscription once, but no longer touches a switch. 12b's test "leaves the switch alone when the assignment fails" goes: the switch no longer depends on the assignment.
- **D9 — The e2e journey (spec gap: "in the platform admin").**
  - The journey switches `families` with `manage("set_features", …)`, the platform admin's command line, through the suite's existing `manage()` helper (`E2E_PYTHON` locally, `E2E_MANAGE` on the staging simulation).
  - It does not use the Django admin UI because no seeded Etqan staff login exists, and the staging simulation has none either. The admin UI is covered by Task 7's Django tests.
  - The spec switches families off and back on inside `try/finally`, so the rest of the suite (and a second run) sees demo as seeded.
  - Plan 12b's `supervision.spec.ts` no longer toggles the checkbox: it makes sure supervision is on with `set_features` and checks the switch is checked and disabled.
- **D10 — Task order.**
  - Tasks 1–7 are backend: registry and storage; supervision into the switchboard with the seeds; routes; per-feature effects; the scanner; `me/` and the admin's read; the platform admin.
  - Tasks 8–10 are the dashboard: `hasFeature`, nav and gates; buttons and sections; Settings → Features.
  - Task 11 is e2e and STATE.md.
  - The seeds sit in Task 2: moving the supervision switch breaks the 12b seed test unless demo's feature is seeded in the same task.

## Review Focus

- **A parent already signed in when Etqan switches parents off.**
  - Expected: a refused sign-in alone is not enough. The next request signs them out, and they stay out once parents are back on, until they sign in again.
  - Tests: Task 4 `test_a_signed_in_parent_is_signed_out_on_the_next_request` and `test_a_parent_cannot_sign_in`.
- **One academy's switch reaching another.**
  - Expected: never, in requests or in the scan (`for_each_academy` loads each academy afresh).
  - Tests: Task 1 `test_one_academys_switches_never_reach_another`; Task 3 `test_each_request_reads_its_own_academys_switches`; Task 5 `test_automatic_notifications_off_skip_only_that_academy` (other academy's pks forced higher with `until_pk_exceeds`).
- **Off, then on again.**
  - Expected: nothing is lost. The family, its students, the payer, the reports and the supervisor come back exactly.
  - Tests: Task 3 `test_the_family_actions_go_with_the_families_feature`; Task 4 `test_the_student_payloads_drop_the_family_and_account_type` and the billing tests; Task 11's e2e (the Omar family is there again).
- **A staff account without the route's code, on a switched-off feature.**
  - Expected: its 403, not a 404 that would reveal nothing but break the permission contract; the dashboard likewise shows "You don't have access" before "not enabled".
  - Tests: Task 3 `test_a_switched_off_feature_is_404_after_the_permission_check` (every feature route); Task 8 "checks the code first, as the server does".
- **Odd stored values: a prerequisite switched off, an unknown code, a non-boolean.**
  - Expected: the feature is held off and says why, the unknown code is ignored, the non-boolean reads as the default, and nothing 500s.
  - Tests: Task 1 `test_a_prerequisite_switched_off_holds_a_feature_off`, `test_unknown_stored_codes_are_ignored_and_the_order_is_the_registrys` and `test_a_missing_key_falls_back_to_the_default`; Task 7 `test_the_change_page_shows_the_features_by_group` (held-off help text) and `test_copying_needs_an_academy_to_copy_from` (junk source).

### Task 1: The feature registry and `Academy.features`

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/etqan/platform/features.py`
- Create (generated): `backend/etqan/tenants/migrations/0003_academy_features.py`
- Modify: `backend/etqan/tenants/models.py`, `backend/conftest.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_features.py` (new), `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_models.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: django-tenants' `connection.tenant` (the request's academy, or `tenant_context`'s).
- Produces:
  - `etqan.platform.features`:
    - `GROUPS: tuple[str, ...]`, `Feature` (frozen dataclass: `code, label_en, label_ar, group, default, built, requires`);
    - `REGISTRY: tuple[Feature, ...]`, `BY_CODE`, `CODES: frozenset[str]`, `BUILT: tuple[str, ...]` (the 8 built codes, in order);
    - `get(code) -> Feature` (raises `LookupError`);
    - `switched_on(code, values) -> bool`, `is_on(code, values) -> bool`, `held_by(code, values) -> list[str]` and `on_codes(values) -> list[str]` (pure; `values` is a stored map or None);
    - `stored() -> dict`, `enabled(code) -> bool`, `enabled_codes() -> list[str]` and `describe() -> list[dict]` (the current academy).
  - `Academy.features: dict` (JSON, `db_default={}`).
  - Root fixture `set_features(*, academy=None, **values)`: switches features on the connection's academy (or `academy`) in the database and on the object; returns that academy; restores `tenants.main` / `tenants.other` afterwards.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The registry matches §3, and the effective value follows §3.3. `enabled()` reads the connection's academy with no query, and one academy's switches never reach another. `information_schema` pins the blue/green-safe column.

Create `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §3, §5.1: the feature registry, the effective value of a switch,
and reading the current academy's switches without a query."""

import re

import pytest
from django.db import connection
from django_tenants.utils import tenant_context

from etqan.platform import features

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db

BUILT = {
    "families": True,
    "parents": True,
    "supervision": False,
    "session_reports": True,
    "invoices": True,
    "auto_notifications": True,
    "teacher_attendance": True,
    "export": True,
}
ARABIC = re.compile(r"[؀-ۿ]")


def test_the_registry_has_36_unique_codes_in_both_languages():
    codes = [feature.code for feature in features.REGISTRY]
    assert len(codes) == 36
    assert len(set(codes)) == 36
    assert set(codes) == features.CODES
    for feature in features.REGISTRY:
        assert re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z_]*", feature.code), feature.code
        assert feature.label_en.strip(), feature.code
        assert ARABIC.search(feature.label_ar), feature.code
        assert feature.group in features.GROUPS, feature.code


def test_the_defaults_match_the_spec():
    """§3.1: eight built features, on by default but supervision; §3.2: the
    other 28 are stored now and off by default."""
    assert {code: features.get(code).default for code in features.BUILT} == BUILT
    assert list(features.BUILT) == list(BUILT)
    unbuilt = [f for f in features.REGISTRY if not f.built]
    assert len(unbuilt) == 28
    assert not any(feature.default for feature in unbuilt)
    assert features.get("report_deductions").requires == ("session_reports",)


def test_requires_names_real_codes_and_has_no_cycles():
    def reach(code, path):
        for needed in features.get(code).requires:
            assert needed in features.CODES, (code, needed)
            assert needed not in path, [*path, needed]
            reach(needed, [*path, needed])

    for feature in features.REGISTRY:
        reach(feature.code, [feature.code])


def test_a_missing_key_falls_back_to_the_default():
    assert features.is_on("families", {}) is True
    assert features.is_on("supervision", None) is False
    assert features.is_on("families", {"families": False}) is False
    assert features.is_on("supervision", {"supervision": True}) is True
    # Only a boolean counts; anything else reads as not stored.
    assert features.is_on("families", {"families": "no"}) is True
    assert features.is_on("supervision", {"supervision": 1}) is False


def test_a_prerequisite_switched_off_holds_a_feature_off():
    both = {"report_deductions": True}
    assert features.is_on("report_deductions", both) is True
    assert features.held_by("report_deductions", both) == []
    held = {"report_deductions": True, "session_reports": False}
    assert features.is_on("report_deductions", held) is False
    assert features.held_by("report_deductions", held) == ["session_reports"]
    # Nothing holds a feature whose own switch is off.
    assert features.held_by("report_deductions", {"session_reports": False}) == []


def test_unknown_stored_codes_are_ignored_and_the_order_is_the_registrys():
    values = {"olovix": True, "levels": True, "families": False}
    assert features.on_codes(values) == [
        "parents",
        "session_reports",
        "invoices",
        "auto_notifications",
        "teacher_attendance",
        "export",
        "levels",
    ]


def test_an_unknown_code_is_a_programming_error():
    with pytest.raises(LookupError, match="Unknown feature: 'olovix'"):
        features.enabled("olovix")
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        features.is_on("olovix", {})


def test_enabled_reads_the_academy_on_the_connection_without_a_query(
    set_features, django_assert_num_queries
):
    set_features(families=False, supervision=True)
    with django_assert_num_queries(0):
        assert features.enabled("families") is False
        assert features.enabled("supervision") is True
        assert features.enabled("parents") is True
        assert "families" not in features.enabled_codes()


def test_one_academys_switches_never_reach_another(tenants, set_features):
    set_features(families=False)
    set_features(academy=tenants.other, invoices=False)
    assert features.enabled("families") is False
    assert features.enabled("invoices") is True
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        assert features.enabled("families") is True
        assert features.enabled("invoices") is False


def test_the_public_schema_reads_the_defaults():
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    assert features.enabled_codes() == [
        code for code, default in BUILT.items() if default
    ]


def test_describe_lists_every_feature_for_the_academy_admin(set_features):
    set_features(report_deductions=True, session_reports=False, levels=True)
    rows = {row["code"]: row for row in features.describe()}
    assert list(rows) == [feature.code for feature in features.REGISTRY]
    assert rows["report_deductions"] == {
        "code": "report_deductions",
        "label_ar": "خصومات التقارير",
        "label_en": "Report deductions",
        "group": "money",
        "on": False,
        "built": False,
        "held_by": ["session_reports"],
    }
    assert rows["levels"]["on"] is True
    assert rows["families"]["on"] is True
    assert rows["families"]["built"] is True
```

```python
def test_the_features_column_is_safe_for_the_old_colour(tenants):
    """Plan 13 §4: a database default, so the old colour's inserts during a
    blue/green deploy still work; an academy starts with nothing stored."""
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute(

            " WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'tenants_academy'"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the registry, the column and the fixture**

The fixture writes with `update()`, not `save()`: django-tenants refuses to save an academy from inside another academy's schema.

Create `backend/etqan/platform/features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13: the academy's feature switches, defined once in code (FT-3).

Etqan switches whole parts of the product on or off for each academy. The
registry below is every switch there is; the values live on the academy
(`Academy.features`, a map from code to boolean in the public schema), and
a missing key means the feature's default. A feature is on when its own
switch is on and every feature it ``requires`` is on too (§3.3).

The platform never imports the tenants app: `enabled` reads the academy
django-tenants has already put on the connection (the request's, or
``for_each_academy``'s), so asking costs no query."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from django.db import connection

GROUPS = ("people", "teaching", "money", "communication", "content", "platform")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Feature:
    code: str
    label_en: str
    label_ar: str
    group: str
    default: bool = False
    # Whether any code checks it yet: the others are stored and editable,
    # and take effect when their phase is built (FT-2).
    built: bool = False
    requires: tuple[str, ...] = ()


def _built(code, label_en, label_ar, group, *, default=True) -> Feature:
    return Feature(code, label_en, label_ar, group, default=default, built=True)


def _later(code, label_en, label_ar, group, *, requires=()) -> Feature:
    return Feature(code, label_en, label_ar, group, requires=requires)


REGISTRY: tuple[Feature, ...] = (
    # §3.1: built, and on by default except General supervision.
    _built("families", "Family accounts", "نظام حسابات العائلات", "people"),
    _built("parents", "Parents and guardians", "نظام أولياء الأمور", "people"),
    _built(
        "supervision",
        "General supervision",
        "نظام الإشراف العام",
        "teaching",
        default=False,
    ),
    _built("session_reports", "Session reports", "نظام تقارير الحصص", "teaching"),
    _built("invoices", "Invoices and receipts", "نظام الفواتير والإيصالات", "money"),
    _built(
        "auto_notifications",
        "Automatic notifications",
        "نظام الإشعارات التلقائية",
        "communication",
    ),
    _built(
        "teacher_attendance",
        "Teachers record attendance",
        "تسجيل المعلم للحضور",
        "teaching",
    ),
    _built("export", "Excel export (CSV)", "نظام تصدير إكسل", "platform"),
    # §3.2: not built yet, off by default.
    _later("levels", "Levels", "نظام المستويات", "teaching"),
    _later("file_uploads", "File uploads", "نظام رفع الملفات", "content"),
    _later("url_redirects", "URL redirects", "نظام تحويل الروابط", "platform"),
    _later("ai_assistant", "AI assistant", "المساعد الذكي", "platform"),
    _later("payment_links", "Payment links", "نظام روابط الدفع", "money"),
    _later(
        "verified_certificates",
        "Verified certificates",
        "نظام الشهادات الموثقة",
        "teaching",
    ),
    _later(
        "incentives_deductions",
        "Incentives and deductions",
        "نظام الحوافز والخصومات",
        "money",
    ),
    _later(
        "free_sessions",
        "Free sessions for subscribers",
        "نظام الحصص المجانية للمشتركين",
        "money",
    ),
    _later("homework", "Homework", "نظام الواجبات", "teaching"),
    _later(
        "internal_chat", "Internal chat", "نظام المحادثات الداخلية", "communication"
    ),
    _later("recorded_courses", "Recorded courses", "نظام الدورات المسجلة", "content"),
    _later("articles", "Articles", "نظام المقالات", "content"),
    _later(
        "country_pricing", "Per-country pricing", "نظام التسعير حسب الدولة", "money"
    ),
    _later("consultations", "Consultations", "نظام الاستشارات", "teaching"),
    _later("study_groups", "Study groups", "نظام المجموعات الدراسية", "teaching"),
    _later("balances", "Balances", "نظام الأرصدة", "money"),
    _later(
        "student_teacher_rate",
        "Per-student teacher rate",
        "سعر المعلم لكل طالب",
        "money",
    ),
    _later("donations", "Donations", "نظام التبرعات", "money"),
    _later("ai_reports", "AI reports", "تقارير الذكاء الاصطناعي", "teaching"),
    _later("contracts", "Contracts", "نظام العقود", "people"),
    _later(
        "subscription_archive", "Subscription archive", "أرشيف الاشتراكات", "teaching"
    ),
    _later("session_archive", "Session archive", "أرشيف الحصص", "teaching"),
    _later(
        "report_deductions",
        "Report deductions",
        "خصومات التقارير",
        "money",
        requires=("session_reports",),
    ),
    _later(
        "fixed_teacher_salary", "Fixed teacher salary", "الراتب الثابت للمعلم", "money"
    ),
    _later(
        "registration_waitlist",
        "Student registration waiting list",
        "قائمة انتظار تسجيل الطلاب",
        "people",
    ),
    _later(
        "payment_receipts", "Payment receipt records", "سجلات إيصالات الدفع", "money"
    ),
    _later("zoom_api", "Zoom API", "ربط Zoom API", "platform"),
    _later("public_links", "Public links", "نظام الروابط العامة", "platform"),
)
BY_CODE = {feature.code: feature for feature in REGISTRY}
CODES = frozenset(BY_CODE)
BUILT = tuple(feature.code for feature in REGISTRY if feature.built)


def get(code: str) -> Feature:
    """The registry's feature; an unknown code is a programming error."""
    try:
        return BY_CODE[code]
    except KeyError:
        raise LookupError(f"Unknown feature: {code!r}") from None


def switched_on(code: str, values: dict | None) -> bool:
    """The feature's own switch: its stored value, or its default when none
    (or a non-boolean) is stored. Prerequisites are not looked at."""
    value = (values or {}).get(code)
    return value if isinstance(value, bool) else get(code).default


def is_on(code: str, values: dict | None) -> bool:
    """§3.3: its own switch is on and so is every feature it requires."""
    return switched_on(code, values) and all(
        is_on(needed, values) for needed in get(code).requires
    )


def held_by(code: str, values: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    """The prerequisites holding a switched-on feature off; empty when its
    own switch is off or nothing holds it."""
    if not switched_on(code, values):
        return []
    return [needed for needed in get(code).requires if not is_on(needed, values)]


def on_codes(values: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    """Every feature that is on, in registry order. Unknown stored codes
    are ignored."""
    return [feature.code for feature in REGISTRY if is_on(feature.code, values)]


def stored() -> dict:
    """The current academy's stored switches, from the academy already on
    the connection: no query. Outside an academy (the public schema) nothing
    is stored, so every feature reads its default."""
    value = getattr(getattr(connection, "tenant", None), "features", None)
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def enabled(code: str) -> bool:
    """Whether ``code`` is on for the current academy (§5.1)."""
    return is_on(code, stored())


def enabled_codes() -> list[str]:
    """The current academy's features that are on, in registry order."""
    return on_codes(stored())


def describe() -> list[dict]:
    """Every feature as the academy admin reads it (§5.6)."""
    values = stored()
    return [
        {
            "code": feature.code,
            "label_ar": feature.label_ar,
            "label_en": feature.label_en,
            "group": feature.group,
            "on": is_on(feature.code, values),
            "built": feature.built,
            "held_by": held_by(feature.code, values),
        }
        for feature in REGISTRY
    ]
```

```python
@pytest.fixture
def set_features(tenants):
    """`set_features(families=False)` switches features (Plan 13) for the
    academy on the connection, or for ``academy=``: written to the database,
    so a request, which loads its academy afresh, sees them too, and onto
    the object, so a service called directly does. The session-shared
    academies get their switches back afterwards (the database rolls back on
    its own)."""
    saved = {name: dict(getattr(tenants, name).features) for name in ("main", "other")}

    def switch(*, academy=None, **values):
        target = academy or connection.tenant
        target.features = {**target.features, **values}
        # An update, not save(): django-tenants refuses to save an academy
        # from inside another academy's schema.
        type(target).objects.filter(pk=target.pk).update(features=target.features)
        return target

    yield switch
    for name, features in saved.items():
        getattr(tenants, name).features = features


@pytest.fixture
def api_for(db):
    """`api_for("teacher")` → an APIClient logged in as a new user of that role.
```

### Task 2: General supervision moves into the switchboard; the seeds

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/etqan/tenants/migrations/0004_supervision_into_features.py` (hand-written)
- Modify:
  - `backend/etqan/academy/api/serializers.py`, `backend/etqan/academy/models.py`, `backend/etqan/academy/services.py`
  - `backend/etqan/scheduling/services/supervision.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/services/__init__.py`
  - `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/payloads.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/session_views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/supervision_views.py`
  - `backend/etqan/tenants/services.py`, `backend/etqan/tenants/management/commands/seed_dev.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/academy/tests/test_api.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/tests/test_api_supervision.py`, `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_features_migration.py` (new), `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_seed_dev.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `features.enabled`, `features.BUILT`, `features.get`, `Academy.features` and the `set_features` fixture.
- Produces:
  - `tenants.services.seed_features(academy, codes) -> list[str]`: switches on the codes with nothing stored, and returns them.
  - `seed_dev.FEATURES = {"demo": features.BUILT}`.
  - The migration's `copy_supervision(apps, schema_editor)`.
  - `academy/settings/`'s `supervision_enabled` becomes read-only (it shows `features.enabled("supervision")`), and `update_settings` no longer takes it.
  - `scheduling.services.supervision_enabled` is **removed**; every caller reads `features.enabled("supervision")`.
- Breaks and updates:
  - `academy/tests/test_api.py`: the two 12b tests of a writable switch become one read-only test.
  - `test_api_supervision.py`: `switch()` becomes a fixture over `set_features`.
  - `test_seed_dev.py`: the two 12b supervision seed tests become a features seed test and a Sara test. The switch no longer follows the assignment, so "leaves the switch alone when the assignment fails" goes.

The migration is tested by calling its function on the test database:
- `supervision(on=…)` sets the 12b column the way a deployed 12b left it;
- the third test drops the column, as an academy mid-deploy would lack it.

Create `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_features_migration.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §4, §8 (Migration): each academy's "General supervision" carries
over into its `supervision` feature; every other academy keeps today's
behaviour (nothing stored, so the defaults)."""

import importlib
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from django.apps import apps
from django.db import connection
from django_tenants.utils import tenant_context

from etqan.academy import services as academy_services
from etqan.tenants.models import Academy

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db

MIGRATION = importlib.import_module(
    "etqan.tenants.migrations.0004_supervision_into_features"
)


def migrate():
    MIGRATION.copy_supervision(apps, SimpleNamespace(connection=connection))


def supervision(*, on: bool) -> None:
    """The current academy's Plan 12b column, as a deployed 12b left it."""
    academy_services.get_settings()
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute(
            "UPDATE academy_academysettings SET supervision_enabled = %s", [on]
        )


def stored(academy) -> dict:
    return Academy.objects.get(pk=academy.pk).features


def test_supervision_carries_over_and_the_rest_keep_the_defaults(tenants):
    supervision(on=True)
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        supervision(on=False)
    migrate()
    assert stored(tenants.main) == {"supervision": True}
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {}
    assert stored(tenants.public) == {}


def test_a_stored_switch_and_a_missing_row_are_left_alone(tenants):
    supervision(on=True)
    Academy.objects.filter(pk=tenants.main.pk).update(
        features={"supervision": False, "families": False}
    )
    # `other` has no settings row yet: nothing to carry.
    with tenant_context(tenants.other), connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute("DELETE FROM academy_academysettings")
    migrate()
    assert stored(tenants.main) == {"supervision": False, "families": False}
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {}


def test_an_academy_without_the_column_yet_is_skipped(tenants):
    """Plan 12b and Plan 13 migrating in one deploy: the public schema runs
    first, before the academy's settings gain the column."""
    supervision(on=True)
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute(
            "ALTER TABLE academy_academysettings DROP COLUMN supervision_enabled"
        )
    migrate()
    assert stored(tenants.main) == {}
```

```python
@pytest.mark.django_db
@override_settings(DEBUG=True)
def test_seed_dev_switches_supervision_on_in_demo_with_sara_on_one_subscription(
    capsys,
):
    from etqan.academy import services as academy  # noqa: PLC0415
    from etqan.scheduling import services as scheduling  # noqa: PLC0415

    def state():
        supervised = scheduling.subscriptions_queryset().filter(
            supervisor__isnull=False
        )
        return (
            academy.get_settings().supervision_enabled,
            [
                (s.student.user.full_name, s.course.name_en, s.supervisor.email)
                for s in supervised
            ],
        )

    call_command("seed_dev")
    call_command("seed_dev")
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    demo = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="demo")
    other = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="other")
    with tenant_context(demo):
        assert state() == (True, [("Yusuf Omar", "Tajweed", "sara@demo.test")])
        # Switched off in a dev database, it stays off.
        academy.update_settings(supervision_enabled=False)
        seed_dev.seed_supervision(seed_dev.SUPERVISION["demo"])
        assert state()[0] is False
    with tenant_context(other):
        assert state() == (False, [])
        capsys.readouterr()
        seed_dev.seed_supervision(seed_dev.SUPERVISION["demo"])
        assert state() == (False, [])
    assert "skip: supervision — sara@demo.test not found" in capsys.readouterr().out


@pytest.mark.django_db
@override_settings(DEBUG=True)
def test_seed_supervision_leaves_the_switch_alone_when_the_assignment_fails(
    monkeypatch, capsys
):
    """M3: the switch must go on only after the assignment succeeds, or a
    skipped assignment (a subscription that can't take a supervisor, say)
    would still leave supervision switched on with nobody assigned."""
    from etqan.academy import services as academy  # noqa: PLC0415
    from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError  # noqa: PLC0415
    from etqan.scheduling import services as scheduling  # noqa: PLC0415

    call_command("seed_dev")
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    demo = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="demo")
    with tenant_context(demo):
        academy.update_settings(supervision_enabled=False)
        # Undo the initial seed's assignment so the "once" guard doesn't
        # short-circuit before reaching the assignment this test forces to
        # fail.
        scheduling.subscriptions_queryset().update(supervisor=None)

        def fails(*args, **kwargs):
            raise ValidationError("nope", field="supervisor_id")

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            seed_dev.scheduling_services, "set_subscription_supervisor", fails
        )
        capsys.readouterr()
        seed_dev.seed_supervision(seed_dev.SUPERVISION["demo"])
        assert academy.get_settings().supervision_enabled is False
        assert "skip: supervision — nope" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

```python
@pytest.mark.django_db
@override_settings(DEBUG=True)
def test_seed_dev_switches_every_built_feature_on_in_demo_only():
    """Plan 13 §7: demo has every built feature on, supervision included;
    other keeps the defaults. A re-run never overrides Etqan's changes."""
    from etqan.platform import features  # noqa: PLC0415

    call_command("seed_dev")
    call_command("seed_dev")
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    demo = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="demo")
    other = Academy.objects.get(subdomain="other")
    assert demo.features == dict.fromkeys(features.BUILT, True)
    assert other.features == {}
    changed = {**demo.features, "families": False, "supervision": False}
    Academy.objects.filter(pk=demo.pk).update(features=changed)
    call_command("seed_dev")
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    assert Academy.objects.get(pk=demo.pk).features == changed
    assert Academy.objects.get(pk=other.pk).features == {}
```

Create `backend/etqan/tenants/migrations/0004_supervision_into_features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §4: each academy's "General supervision" (Plan 12b's
`AcademySettings.supervision_enabled`, in the academy's own schema) becomes
its `supervision` feature.

It runs in the public schema, before any academy's own migrations. An
academy whose settings have no such column yet (Plan 12b migrating in the
same deploy) had supervision off, the default, and is skipped; so is one
with no settings row, and one whose switch is already stored."""

from django.db import migrations
from django_tenants.utils import get_public_schema_name

HAS_COLUMN = (
    "SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema = %s"
    " AND table_name = 'academy_academysettings'"
    " AND column_name = 'supervision_enabled'"
)


def copy_supervision(apps, schema_editor):
    academy_model = apps.get_model("tenants", "Academy")
    connection = schema_editor.connection
    academies = academy_model.objects.exclude(schema_name=get_public_schema_name())
    with connection.cursor() as cursor:
        for academy in academies.order_by("pk"):
            if "supervision" in academy.features:
                continue
            cursor.execute(HAS_COLUMN, [academy.schema_name])
            if cursor.fetchone() is None:
                continue
            table = f"{connection.ops.quote_name(academy.schema_name)}.academy_academysettings"
            cursor.execute(
                f"SELECT supervision_enabled FROM {table} ORDER BY id LIMIT 1"  # noqa: S608
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is not None and row[0]:
                academy_model.objects.filter(pk=academy.pk).update(
                    features={**academy.features, "supervision": True}
                )


class Migration(migrations.Migration):
    dependencies = [
        ("tenants", "0003_academy_features"),
    ]

    operations = [
        migrations.RunPython(copy_supervision, migrations.RunPython.noop),
    ]
```

```python
def seed_features(academy: Academy, codes) -> list[str]:
    """Dev seeds (Plan 13 §7): switch ``codes`` on where nothing is stored
    for them yet, so a re-run never overrides a switch Etqan changed.
    Returns the codes it switched on."""
    missing = [code for code in codes if code not in academy.features]
    for code in missing:
        features.get(code)  # an unknown code is a programming error
    if missing:
        academy.features = {**academy.features, **dict.fromkeys(missing, True)}
        Academy.objects.filter(pk=academy.pk).update(features=academy.features)
    return missing


def set_status(academy_ids: list[int], status: str) -> int:
    if status not in Academy.Status.values:
        raise ValidationError("Unknown status.", field="status")
```

### Task 3: Routes declare their feature; the route table's feature column

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `backend/etqan/platform/permissions.py`
  - `backend/etqan/identity/api/people_views.py`, `backend/etqan/billing/api/views.py`
  - `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/session_views.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/supervision_views.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_feature_on.py` (new), `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_routes.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/tests/test_family_bulk.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `features.enabled` and the `set_features` fixture.
- Produces:
  - `platform.permissions.FEATURE_OFF = "This feature is not enabled for this academy."`.
  - `required_feature(view, request) -> str | None` reads a view's `feature`: a code, or a method → code map (HEAD as GET).
  - `FeatureOn`, listed after the role/code check, raises `NotFound(FEATURE_OFF)`.
  - The views' `feature` declarations (D3), and `test_routes.FEATURES` / `FEATURE_WORDS`.
- Breaks and updates: `supervision_views.require_supervision` and the session PATCH's own 404 go; their views declare `feature` instead.

`test_routes.py` gains the feature column and three tests:
- the column matches the views, and no feature route is left undeclared;
- every feature route 404s after the permission check, for an admin and for staff holding the code, while a staff account without it keeps its 403;
- every other route is untouched with every built feature off.

The bulk family actions get their own test.

```python
    return match.route, required_code(view, SimpleNamespace(method=method))


def _declared_feature(method: str, path: str):
    """The feature the view behind ``path`` declares for ``method``, and
    whether it lists the class that enforces it."""
    match = resolve(path)
    view = match.func.cls()
    view.kwargs, view.args = match.kwargs, ()
    feature = required_feature(view, SimpleNamespace(method=method))
    return feature, FeatureOn in view.permission_classes


def _qualname(cls) -> str:
    return f"{cls.__module__}.{cls.__qualname__}"
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize(("method", "path", "code"), ROUTES)
def test_admins_pass_every_route(api_for, method, path, code):
    assert _call(api_for("admin"), method, path).status_code != 403


def _tabled_paths():
    yield from ((method, path) for method, path, _ in ROUTES)
    yield from ADMIN_ONLY


def test_every_route_of_a_built_feature_declares_it():
    """Plan 13 §5.2: the feature column matches what each view declares; a
    route left undeclared, or declaring a code the registry lacks (or that
    is not built), fails here."""
    tabled = set(_tabled_paths())
    assert set(FEATURES) <= tabled
    for method, path in tabled:
        declared, enforced = _declared_feature(method, path)
        assert declared == FEATURES.get((method, path)), (method, path)
        if declared is not None:
            assert declared in features.BUILT, (method, path, declared)
            assert enforced, (method, path)
        for word, feature in FEATURE_WORDS.items():
            if word in path:
                assert FEATURES.get((method, path)) == feature, (method, path)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("method", "path"), sorted(FEATURES))
def test_a_switched_off_feature_is_404_after_the_permission_check(
    api_for, staff_for, set_features, method, path
):
    set_features(**{FEATURES[(method, path)]: False})
    code = next(code for m, p, code in ROUTES if (m, p) == (method, path))
    codes = (code,) if isinstance(code, str) else code
    without = staff_for(*sorted(registry.CODES - set(codes)))
    assert _call(without, method, path).status_code == 403
    for client in (api_for("admin"), staff_for(*codes)):
        resp = _call(client, method, path)
        assert (resp.status_code, resp.json()) == (404, {"detail": FEATURE_OFF})


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("method", "path"),
    [(m, p) for m, p in _tabled_paths() if (m, p) not in FEATURES],
)
def test_every_other_route_is_there_with_every_feature_off(
    api_for, set_features, method, path
):
    set_features(**dict.fromkeys(features.BUILT, False))
    resp = _call(api_for("admin"), method, path)
    assert resp.status_code != 403
    assert resp.status_code != 404 or resp.json() != {"detail": FEATURE_OFF}
```

Create `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_feature_on.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.2: a view declares its feature, and `FeatureOn` answers 404
for it while the academy has it switched off, request by request."""

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from django_tenants.utils import tenant_context
from rest_framework.exceptions import NotFound

from etqan.platform.permissions import FEATURE_OFF
from etqan.platform.permissions import FeatureOn
from etqan.platform.permissions import required_feature

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
OTHER_HOST = "pytest-other.etqan.localhost"
FAMILIES = "/api/v1/people/families/"


class _Family:
    feature = "families"


class _Session:
    feature = {"PATCH": "supervision"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("view", "method", "expected"),
    [
        (_Family(), "GET", "families"),
        (_Family(), "POST", "families"),
        (_Session(), "PATCH", "supervision"),
        (_Session(), "GET", None),
        (_Session(), "HEAD", None),
        (object(), "GET", None),
    ],
)
def test_the_view_declares_its_feature(view, method, expected):
    assert required_feature(view, SimpleNamespace(method=method)) == expected


def test_feature_on_refuses_only_a_switched_off_feature(set_features):
    check = FeatureOn()
    request = SimpleNamespace(method="PATCH")
    set_features(supervision=True)
    assert check.has_permission(request, _Session()) is True
    assert check.has_permission(request, object()) is True
    set_features(supervision=False)
    with pytest.raises(NotFound, match=FEATURE_OFF):
        check.has_permission(request, _Session())
    assert check.has_permission(SimpleNamespace(method="GET"), _Session()) is True


def test_each_request_reads_its_own_academys_switches(api_for, tenants, set_features):
    """One academy's switch never reaches another's requests."""
    ours = api_for("admin")
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        theirs = api_for("admin")
    set_features(academy=tenants.other, families=False)
    assert ours.get(FAMILIES).status_code == 200
    resp = theirs.get(FAMILIES, HTTP_HOST=OTHER_HOST)
    assert (resp.status_code, resp.json()) == (404, {"detail": FEATURE_OFF})
    set_features(academy=tenants.other, families=True)
    set_features(academy=tenants.main, families=False)
    assert ours.get(FAMILIES).status_code == 404
    assert theirs.get(FAMILIES, HTTP_HOST=OTHER_HOST).status_code == 200
```

Expected: FAIL — the run stops with 2 collection errors: `FEATURE_OFF`, `FeatureOn` and `required_feature` don't exist in `etqan.platform.permissions`.

Guards: once they exist, `test_every_other_route_is_there_with_every_feature_off` passes before any view declares a feature. It guards against over-reaching.

`FeatureOn` raises rather than returning False, so DRF answers 404, not 403. It goes last in `permission_classes`, so the role/code check runs first.

```python
        wanted = (code,) if isinstance(code, str) else code or ()
        held = codes_of(request.user)
        return any(c in held for c in wanted)


FEATURE_OFF = "This feature is not enabled for this academy."


def required_feature(view, request) -> str | None:
    """The feature ``view`` belongs to for this request (Plan 13 §5.2): its
    ``feature``, or by method from a map (HEAD as GET). None: a route every
    academy has."""
    feature = getattr(view, "feature", None)
    if isinstance(feature, dict):
        method = "GET" if request.method == "HEAD" else request.method
        return feature.get(method)
    return feature


class FeatureOn(BasePermission):
    """Plan 13 §5.2: a route of a switched-off feature answers 404, as a
    route that isn't there. It is listed after the role and code check
    (``[HasCode, FeatureOn]``), so a caller that check refuses still gets
    its 403. Views declare ``feature`` (a code, or method → code)."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        code = required_feature(view, request)
        if code is not None and not features.enabled(code):
            raise NotFound(FEATURE_OFF)
        return True
```

### Task 4: What each switched-off feature does beyond its routes

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/etqan/identity/authentication.py`
- Modify:
  - `backend/config/settings/base.py`
  - `backend/etqan/identity/services.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/payloads.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/people_filters.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/people_views.py`
  - `backend/etqan/billing/services/payers.py`, `backend/etqan/billing/services/subscriptions.py`
  - `backend/etqan/scheduling/api/payloads.py`, `backend/etqan/scheduling/services/attendance.py`
  - `backend/etqan/platform/csv.py`
- Test:
  - new: `backend/etqan/identity/tests/test_features.py`, `backend/etqan/billing/tests/test_features.py`, `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_export_feature.py`
  - `backend/etqan/scheduling/tests/test_api_session_writes.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `features.enabled` and `set_features`; Task 3's `FEATURE_OFF`.
- Produces:
  - `identity.services.PARENT_ACCESS_OFF` and `sign_in_refusal(user) -> str | None`.
  - `identity.authentication.SessionAuthentication`, the API's default authentication class.
  - `people_views.FAMILY_COLUMNS`.
  - `scheduling.services.attendance.TEACHER_ATTENDANCE_OFF`.
  - The effects in D4:
    - the student payload, filters and CSV (`families`);
    - the payer rule (`families`, `parents`), sign-in and `me/` children (`parents`);
    - automatic invoices and the subscriptions' payment status (`invoices`);
    - teachers' attendance (`teacher_attendance`) and `?format=csv` (`export`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Each test switches the feature off, checks the effect, and switches it back on to find everything as it was.

Create `backend/etqan/billing/tests/test_features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.3: billing while the academy's switches are off. The payer
rule skips the family payer (family accounts off) and the guardians
(parents off); with invoices off a subscription gets no invoice, whatever
the auto-invoice setting, and no payment status. Switched back on,
everything is as it was."""

import pytest

from etqan.billing import services
from etqan.billing.models import Invoice
from etqan.billing.tests.conftest import make_parent
from etqan.billing.tests.test_subscription_api import URL
from etqan.billing.tests.test_subscription_api import subscribe
from etqan.identity import services as identity_services
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import make_student

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db


def options(student):
    return [(p.full_name, r) for p, r in services.payer_options(student.pk)]


def test_family_accounts_off_skip_the_family_payer(world, set_features):
    yusuf = world.student
    aisha = make_student("Aisha")
    make_parent("Zainab", yusuf)
    identity_services.create_family(
        name="Family", student_ids=[yusuf.pk, aisha.pk], payer_id=aisha.pk
    )
    set_features(families=False)
    assert options(yusuf) == [("Zainab", "guardian"), ("Yusuf", "student")]
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as refused:
        services.resolve_payer(yusuf.pk, aisha.pk)
    assert refused.value.field == "payer"
    set_features(families=True)
    assert options(yusuf)[0] == ("Aisha", "family")


def test_parents_off_skip_the_guardians(world, set_features):
    yusuf = world.student
    make_parent("Zainab", yusuf)
    set_features(parents=False)
    assert options(yusuf) == [("Yusuf", "student")]
    assert services.resolve_payer(yusuf.pk, None) == yusuf
    set_features(parents=True)
    assert options(yusuf) == [("Zainab", "guardian"), ("Yusuf", "student")]


def test_invoices_off_mean_no_automatic_invoice_and_no_payment_status(
    api_for, world, set_features
):
    admin = api_for("admin")
    set_features(invoices=False)
    sub = subscribe(admin, world, price_minor=120000)
    assert "payment_status" not in sub
    renewal = admin.post(f"{URL}{sub['id']}/renew/", {}, format="json").json()
    assert not Invoice.objects.filter(
        subscription_id__in=[sub["id"], renewal["id"]]
    ).exists()
    assert "payment_status" not in admin.get(URL).json()["results"][0]
    set_features(invoices=True)
    again = subscribe(admin, world, price_minor=120000)
    assert again["payment_status"] == "unpaid"
    assert Invoice.objects.filter(subscription_id=again["id"]).count() == 1
```

Create `backend/etqan/identity/tests/test_features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.3: people while the academy's switches are off. Family
accounts off hide a student's family and account type; parents off keep
parent accounts from signing in and drop `me/`'s children. Nothing is
deleted: switched back on, everything is as it was."""

import csv
import io

import pytest
from rest_framework.test import APIClient

from etqan.identity import services
from etqan.identity.models import Guardianship
from etqan.identity.models import ParentProfile

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
STUDENTS = "/api/v1/people/students/"
LOGIN = "/api/v1/identity/login/"
ME = "/api/v1/identity/me/"
PASSWORD = "sup3r-secret-pw"
PARENTS_OFF = "Parent access is not enabled for this academy."


def student(name):
    return services.create_person("student", full_name=name)


def parent(name, email, *children):
    user = services.create_person("parent", full_name=name, email=email, invite=False)
    user.set_password(PASSWORD)
    user.save()
    for child in children:
        services.link_guardian(user, child)
    return user


def signed_in(email):
    client = APIClient()
    resp = client.post(LOGIN, {"email": email, "password": PASSWORD}, format="json")
    return client, resp


class TestFamilyAccountsOff:
    def test_the_student_payloads_drop_the_family_and_account_type(
        self, api_for, set_features
    ):
        yusuf, aisha = student("Yusuf"), student("Aisha")
        services.create_family(
            name="Omar family", student_ids=[yusuf.pk, aisha.pk], payer_id=aisha.pk
        )
        admin = api_for("admin")
        set_features(families=False)
        for profile in (
            admin.get(f"{STUDENTS}{yusuf.pk}/").json()["profile"],
            admin.get(STUDENTS).json()["results"][0]["profile"],
        ):
            assert "family" not in profile
            assert "account_type" not in profile
        set_features(families=True)
        profile = admin.get(f"{STUDENTS}{yusuf.pk}/").json()["profile"]
        assert profile["account_type"] == "family"
        assert profile["family"]["name"] == "Omar family"

    def test_the_family_filters_are_ignored(self, api_for, set_features):
        yusuf = student("Yusuf")
        student("Aisha")
        family = services.create_family(
            name="F", student_ids=[yusuf.pk], payer_id=yusuf.pk
        )
        admin = api_for("admin")

        def names(**params):
            rows = admin.get(STUDENTS, params).json()["results"]
            return sorted(row["user"]["full_name"] for row in rows)

        assert names(family=family.pk) == ["Yusuf"]
        assert names(account_type="individual") == ["Aisha"]
        set_features(families=False)
        assert names(family=family.pk) == ["Aisha", "Yusuf"]
        assert names(account_type="individual") == ["Aisha", "Yusuf"]

    def test_the_students_csv_has_no_family_columns(self, api_for, set_features):
        student("Yusuf")
        admin = api_for("admin")

        def header():
            body = admin.get(STUDENTS, {"format": "csv"}).content.decode("utf-8-sig")
            return next(csv.reader(io.StringIO(body)))

        assert header()[-2:] == ["Account type", "Family"]
        set_features(families=False)
        assert "Account type" not in header()
        assert "Family" not in header()


class TestParentsOff:
    def test_a_parent_cannot_sign_in(self, set_features):
        yusuf = student("Yusuf")
        parent("Zainab", "zainab@x.test", yusuf)
        set_features(parents=False)
        client, resp = signed_in("zainab@x.test")
        assert (resp.status_code, resp.json()) == (403, {"detail": PARENTS_OFF})
        assert client.get(ME).status_code == 403
        set_features(parents=True)
        client, resp = signed_in("zainab@x.test")
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert [child["full_name"] for child in resp.json()["children"]] == ["Yusuf"]

    def test_a_signed_in_parent_is_signed_out_on_the_next_request(self, set_features):
        parent("Zainab", "zainab@x.test", student("Yusuf"))
        client, resp = signed_in("zainab@x.test")
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        set_features(parents=False)
        assert client.get(ME).status_code == 403
        # Signed out, not only refused: it stays out once parents are back on.
        set_features(parents=True)
        assert client.get(ME).status_code == 403

    def test_everyone_else_still_signs_in_and_me_has_no_children(
        self, api_for, set_features
    ):
        """A teacher who is also a guardian keeps signing in; `me/` drops
        the children while parents are off."""
        yusuf = student("Yusuf")
        teacher = services.create_person(
            "teacher",
            full_name="Bilal",
            email="bilal@x.test",
            profile={"gender": "male"},
            invite=False,
        )
        teacher.set_password(PASSWORD)
        teacher.save()
        Guardianship.objects.create(
            parent=ParentProfile.objects.create(user=teacher),
            student=services.get_student_profile(yusuf.pk),
        )
        set_features(parents=False)
        client, resp = signed_in("bilal@x.test")
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert "children" not in resp.json()
        assert "children" not in client.get(ME).json()
        set_features(parents=True)
        assert [c["full_name"] for c in client.get(ME).json()["children"]] == ["Yusuf"]
```

Create `backend/etqan/platform/tests/test_export_feature.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.3: with export switched off, every `?format=csv` answers 404;
the lists themselves stay."""

import pytest

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db

CSV_LISTS = [
    "/api/v1/people/students/",
    "/api/v1/people/parents/",
    "/api/v1/people/teachers/",
    "/api/v1/people/admins/",
    "/api/v1/catalogue/courses/",
    "/api/v1/catalogue/packages/",
    "/api/v1/subscriptions/",
    "/api/v1/sessions/",
    "/api/v1/billing/invoices/",
    "/api/v1/payroll/payslips/",
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url", CSV_LISTS)
def test_export_off_turns_every_csv_into_a_404(api_for, set_features, url):
    admin = api_for("admin")
    resp = admin.get(url, {"format": "csv"})
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp["Content-Type"].startswith("text/csv")
    set_features(export=False)
    assert admin.get(url, {"format": "csv"}).status_code == 404
    assert admin.get(url).status_code == 200
    set_features(export=True)
    assert admin.get(url, {"format": "csv"}).status_code == 200
```

Run: `.venv/bin/pytest etqan/billing/tests/test_features.py etqan/identity/tests/test_features.py etqan/platform/tests/test_export_feature.py etqan/scheduling/tests/test_api_session_writes.py -q`

Expected: FAIL — 20 failed, 29 passed. The 20 are every new test: the 3 billing tests, the 6 people tests, the 10 CSV lists (still 200 with export off) and the teacher attendance test. The 29 are `test_api_session_writes.py`'s existing tests.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

A parent signed in before parents went off is signed out, not refused. The authentication class treats them as anonymous, so the CSRF and sign-in routes keep working for that browser, and every other route answers 403.

```python
    invoices, the default first: the family payer, then the active guardians
    (the earliest-linked first), then the student. Someone who qualifies
    twice appears once, under their first relation. Three queries whatever
```

```python
    """``(person, relation)`` for each person who may pay a student's
    invoices, the default first: the family payer, then the active guardians
    (the earliest-linked first), then the student. Someone who qualifies
    twice appears once, under their first relation. At most three queries
    whatever the family's size."""
    family_payer = (
```

Create `backend/etqan/identity/authentication.py`:

```python
"""The API's session authentication (Plan 13 §5.3): a signed-in account the
academy no longer lets in (a parent, once parents are switched off) is
signed out on its next request, and treated as anonymous."""

from django.contrib.auth import logout
from rest_framework import authentication

from etqan.identity import services


class SessionAuthentication(authentication.SessionAuthentication):
    def authenticate(self, request):
        result = super().authenticate(request)
        if result is not None and services.sign_in_refusal(result[0]) is not None:
            # The Django request holds the session; DRF's wraps it.
            logout(request._request)  # noqa: SLF001
            return None
        return result
```

```python


class CSVExportMixin:
    """Lets a list view answer `GET ?format=csv` with ``csv_response(rows)``,
    while the academy has export on (Plan 13 §5.3): with it off there is no
    CSV renderer, so DRF answers any ``?format=csv`` with a 404."""

    csv_columns: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()
    csv_filename = "export"

    def get_renderers(self):
        renderers = super().get_renderers()
        if self.request.method == "GET" and features.enabled("export"):
            renderers.append(CSVRenderer())
        return renderers
```
