### Task 5: The notifications scanner follows each academy's switches

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/etqan/notifications/services/scan.py`, `backend/etqan/notifications/recipients.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/notifications/tests/test_features.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `features.enabled` and `set_features`; `tasks.scan` (`for_each_academy`), `until_pk_exceeds`.
- Produces:
  - `scan.FEATURE_OF`: `report.missing` → `session_reports`; `invoice.issued` and `invoice.overdue` → `invoices`.
  - `scan_now()` returns 0 while `auto_notifications` is off.
  - `recipients.resolve` leaves the guardians out while `parents` is off.
- Notifications stays the only place that decides who is told what (STATE.md's standing warning): nothing outside `etqan.notifications` changes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The isolation test switches automatic notifications off in `main` only, and runs the real beat job over both academies. `other`'s rows are forced above 10 000 so a leak would show.

Create `backend/etqan/notifications/tests/test_features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.3: the scanner follows each academy's switches. Automatic
notifications off skip the academy entirely; session reports off send no
`report.missing`, invoices off no invoice notices, and parents off tell no
guardian. One academy's switches never reach another's scan."""

from datetime import UTC
from datetime import date
from datetime import datetime
from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from django_tenants.utils import tenant_context
from rest_framework.test import APIClient

from etqan.billing import services as billing_services
from etqan.notifications import services
from etqan.notifications import tasks
from etqan.notifications.models import Notification
from etqan.notifications.tests.conftest import build_family
from etqan.notifications.tests.conftest import lesson
from etqan.notifications.tests.conftest import notice
from etqan.scheduling import services as scheduling_services
from etqan.scheduling.tests.conftest import until_pk_exceeds

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
TEN = datetime(2026, 6, 1, 10, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
REMINDER_AT = TEN - timedelta(minutes=60)


def told(type_=None):
    rows = Notification.objects.select_related("recipient")
    if type_ is not None:
        rows = rows.filter(type=type_)
    return sorted(row.recipient.full_name for row in rows)


def test_automatic_notifications_off_skip_only_that_academy(
    family, clock, tenants, set_features
):
    here = lesson(family)
    sent = notice(family.omar, target_id=here.pk, type_="session.early_reminder")
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        there_family = build_family()
        # The other academy's rows get higher pks than any row here.
        until_pk_exceeds(Notification, 10_000, lambda: None)
        there = lesson(there_family)
    set_features(academy=tenants.main, auto_notifications=False)
    clock.set(REMINDER_AT)
    assert tasks.scan() == {
        tenants.main.schema_name: "ok",
        tenants.other.schema_name: "ok",
    }
    assert list(Notification.objects.all()) == [sent]
    with tenant_context(tenants.other):
        theirs = Notification.objects.filter(type="session.reminder")
        assert {row.target_id for row in theirs} == {there.pk}
        assert min(row.pk for row in theirs) > 10_000
    # The bell still shows what was sent before.
    omar = APIClient()
    omar.force_login(family.omar)
    bell = omar.get("/api/v1/notifications/").json()["results"]
    assert [row["id"] for row in bell] == [sent.pk]
    set_features(academy=tenants.main, auto_notifications=True)
    tasks.scan()
    assert told("session.reminder") == ["Huda", "Omar", "Yusuf"]


def test_session_reports_off_send_no_missing_report(family, clock, set_features):
    session = lesson(family)
    clock.set(TEN + timedelta(minutes=10))
    scheduling_services.mark_attendance(
        session, by=family.admin, student_attendance="present"
    )
    later = TEN + timedelta(minutes=45) + timedelta(hours=24, minutes=1)
    set_features(session_reports=False)
    services.scan(now=later)
    assert told("report.missing") == []
    set_features(session_reports=True)
    services.scan(now=later)
    assert told("report.missing") == ["Bilal"]


def test_invoices_off_send_no_invoice_notices(family, set_features):
    billing_services.create_invoice(
        student_id=family.student.id,
        amount_minor=150000,
        due_on=date(2026, 6, 8),
        description="Tajweed",
        by=family.admin,
    )
    overdue = datetime(2026, 6, 16, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
    set_features(invoices=False)
    services.scan(now=datetime.now(tz=UTC))
    services.scan(now=overdue)
    assert told("invoice.issued") == told("invoice.overdue") == []
    set_features(invoices=True)
    services.scan(now=datetime.now(tz=UTC))
    services.scan(now=overdue)
    assert told("invoice.issued") == ["Omar"]
    assert told("invoice.overdue") == ["Amina", "Omar"]


def test_parents_off_tell_no_guardian(family, set_features):
    lesson(family)
    set_features(parents=False)
    services.scan(now=REMINDER_AT)
    assert told("session.reminder") == ["Yusuf"]
    set_features(parents=True)
    services.scan(now=REMINDER_AT)
    assert told("session.reminder") == ["Huda", "Omar", "Yusuf"]
```

```python
def resolve(hits: list[Hit]) -> list[tuple[Hit, object]]:
    """``(hit, user)`` for every eligible recipient of every hit, each user
    once per hit, in audience order."""
    students = {hit.student.pk for hit in hits if finders.GUARDIANS in hit.audience}
    guardians = identity_services.guardians_by_student(students) if students else {}
    wants_admins = any(finders.ADMINS in hit.audience for hit in hits)
    admins = identity_services.active_admins() if wants_admins else []
```

with:

```python

def resolve(hits: list[Hit]) -> list[tuple[Hit, object]]:
    """``(hit, user)`` for every eligible recipient of every hit, each user
    once per hit, in audience order. Guardians are told only while the
    academy has parents on (Plan 13 §5.3)."""
    students = {hit.student.pk for hit in hits if finders.GUARDIANS in hit.audience}
    if not features.enabled("parents"):
        students = set()
    guardians = identity_services.guardians_by_student(students) if students else {}
    wants_admins = any(finders.ADMINS in hit.audience for hit in hits)
    admins = identity_services.active_admins() if wants_admins else []
```

### Task 6: `me/` lists the features that are on; the admin's `academy/features/`

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/etqan/identity/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/identity/api/serializers.py`, `backend/etqan/academy/api/views.py`, `backend/etqan/academy/api/urls.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/identity/tests/test_features.py`, `backend/etqan/academy/tests/test_features_api.py` (new), `backend/etqan/access/tests/test_routes.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `features.enabled_codes()` and `features.describe()`; the route table's `ADMIN_ONLY`.
- Produces:
  - `me/` (and the login response) gains `features: list[str]`, the codes that are on, in registry order.
  - `GET /api/v1/academy/features/` returns `[{code, label_ar, label_en, group, on, built, held_by}]`, admins only (`AcademyFeaturesView`).
- Breaks and updates: `test_routes.ADMIN_ONLY` gains `("GET", "/api/v1/academy/features/")`. Without it the coverage test fails on an unlisted route.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `backend/etqan/academy/tests/test_features_api.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.6: the academy's admin reads every feature and whether it is
on; nothing in the academy's API changes them (§5.5)."""

import pytest
from django.db import connection
from rest_framework.test import APIClient

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
URL = "/api/v1/academy/features/"


def test_the_admin_reads_every_feature_in_registry_order(api_for, set_features):
    set_features(families=False, report_deductions=True, session_reports=False)
    rows = api_for("admin").get(URL).json()
    assert len(rows) == 36
    assert rows[0] == {
        "code": "families",
        "label_ar": "نظام حسابات العائلات",
        "label_en": "Family accounts",
        "group": "people",
        "on": False,
        "built": True,
        "held_by": [],
    }
    by_code = {row["code"]: row for row in rows}
    assert (by_code["parents"]["on"], by_code["supervision"]["on"]) == (True, False)
    assert by_code["report_deductions"]["held_by"] == ["session_reports"]
    assert by_code["levels"] == {
        "code": "levels",
        "label_ar": "نظام المستويات",
        "label_en": "Levels",
        "group": "teaching",
        "on": False,
        "built": False,
        "held_by": [],
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role", ["teacher", "student", "parent"])
def test_only_admins_read_them(api_for, staff_for, role):
    assert api_for(role).get(URL).status_code == 403
    # Admins only: no staff code opens it (the route table's ADMIN_ONLY
    # proves it for every code; this academy's settings codes here).
    editor = staff_for("academy_settings.view", "academy_settings.update")
    assert editor.get(URL).status_code == 403
    assert APIClient().get(URL).status_code == 403


def test_nothing_in_the_academys_api_writes_them(api_for):
    admin = api_for("admin")
    for method in ("post", "put", "patch", "delete"):
        resp = getattr(admin, method)(URL, {"families": False}, format="json")
        assert resp.status_code == 405, method
    resp = admin.patch(
        "/api/v1/academy/settings/",
        {"features": {"families": False}, "supervision_enabled": True},
        format="json",
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    connection.tenant.refresh_from_db()
    assert connection.tenant.features == {}
```

### Task 7: The platform admin: Features on the Academy page, copying, history; `set_features`

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/etqan/tenants/management/commands/set_features.py`
- Modify: `backend/etqan/tenants/admin.py`, `backend/etqan/tenants/services.py`
- Test: `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_admin_features.py` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's registry (`REGISTRY`, `GROUPS`, `switched_on`, `held_by`, `on_codes`, `CODES`), `platform.params.as_int`.
- Produces:
  - `tenants.services.set_features(academy, values: dict[str, bool]) -> Academy`: a 400 on `features` for an unknown code, and nothing is written.
  - `tenants.services.copy_features(source, targets) -> list[Academy]`.
  - `admin.AcademyChangeForm` (a `feature_<code>` checkbox per feature), `AcademyActionForm` (`copy_from`) and the `copy_features` admin action.
  - `manage.py set_features <subdomain> [--on CODE …] [--off CODE …]`, which prints `<subdomain>: code=on, code=off`. Task 11's e2e runs it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`submitted(resp)` posts the change page back the way a browser would: an unchecked box is left out, and the read-only domains' inline goes back as rendered.

Create `backend/etqan/tenants/tests/test_admin_features.py`:

```python
"""Plan 13 §5.5: Etqan switches an academy's features on its change page in
the platform admin (the bare base domain), copies them from another academy,
and every change is in the admin's history; so does `set_features`."""

import pytest
from django import forms
from django.contrib.admin.models import LogEntry
from django.core.management import call_command
from django.core.management.base import CommandError
from django.db import connection

from etqan.identity.models import User
from etqan.tenants.models import Academy

pytestmark = pytest.mark.django_db
PUBLIC_HOST = "etqan.localhost"


@pytest.fixture
def staff_client(client):
    connection.set_schema_to_public()
    staff = User.objects.create_superuser(
        email="ops3@etqan.test", password="pw-12345678"
    )
    client.force_login(staff)
    return client


def change_url(academy) -> str:
    return f"/admin/tenants/academy/{academy.pk}/change/"


def stored(academy) -> dict:
    return Academy.objects.get(pk=academy.pk).features


def submitted(resp) -> dict:
    """The change form's data as the page would post it: an unchecked box is
    left out, and the domains' inline goes back as it came."""
    form = resp.context["adminform"].form
    data = {}
    for name, field in form.fields.items():
        value = form[name].value()
        if isinstance(field, forms.BooleanField):
            if value:
                data[name] = "on"
        elif value is not None:
            data[name] = value
    for inline in resp.context["inline_admin_formsets"]:
        formset = inline.formset
        management = formset.management_form
        for name in management.fields:
            data[f"{formset.prefix}-{name}"] = management[name].value()
        for row in formset.forms:
            for name in row.fields:
                if (value := row[name].value()) is not None:
                    data[row.add_prefix(name)] = value
    return data


def test_the_change_page_shows_the_features_by_group(staff_client, tenants):
    Academy.objects.filter(pk=tenants.other.pk).update(
        features={"report_deductions": True, "session_reports": False}
    )
    resp = staff_client.get(change_url(tenants.other), HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST)
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    titles = [fieldset.name for fieldset in resp.context["adminform"]]
    assert titles == [
        None,
        "Features: People",
        "Features: Teaching",
        "Features: Money",
        "Features: Communication",
        "Features: Content",
        "Features: Platform",
    ]
    form = resp.context["adminform"].form
    assert form["feature_families"].value() is True
    assert form["feature_supervision"].value() is False
    assert form["feature_session_reports"].value() is False
    assert form.fields["feature_families"].label == (
        "Family accounts (نظام حسابات العائلات)"
    )
    assert form.fields["feature_levels"].help_text == "Takes effect when built."
    assert form.fields["feature_report_deductions"].help_text == (
        "Takes effect when built. Held off: needs Session reports."
    )
    assert "Only Etqan switches these" in resp.content.decode()


def test_etqan_switches_a_feature_and_the_history_records_it(staff_client, tenants):
    url = change_url(tenants.other)
    data = submitted(staff_client.get(url, HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST))
    del data["feature_families"]
    data["feature_supervision"] = "on"
    resp = staff_client.post(url, data, HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST)
    assert resp.status_code == 302, resp.context["adminform"].form.errors
    # Only what changed is stored: the rest keep following their defaults.
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {"families": False, "supervision": True}
    entry = LogEntry.objects.filter(object_id=str(tenants.other.pk)).latest("pk")
    message = entry.get_change_message()
    assert "Family accounts" in message
    assert "General supervision" in message
    # Saved again untouched, nothing more is stored.
    data = submitted(staff_client.get(url, HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST))
    staff_client.post(url, data, HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST)
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {"families": False, "supervision": True}


def test_etqan_copies_features_from_another_academy(staff_client, tenants):
    Academy.objects.filter(pk=tenants.main.pk).update(
        features={"families": False, "levels": True}
    )
    resp = staff_client.post(
        "/admin/tenants/academy/",
        {
            "action": "copy_features",
            "_selected_action": [tenants.other.pk, tenants.main.pk],
            "copy_from": tenants.main.pk,
        },
        HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST,
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 302
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {"families": False, "levels": True}
    assert stored(tenants.main) == {"families": False, "levels": True}
    entry = LogEntry.objects.filter(object_id=str(tenants.other.pk)).latest("pk")
    assert entry.get_change_message() == "Copied features from Pytest Main Academy."
    assert not LogEntry.objects.filter(object_id=str(tenants.main.pk)).exists()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("copy_from", "message"),
    [
        ("", "Choose the academy to copy the features from."),
        # Junk never reaches the action: the action bar refuses it.
        ("x", "No action selected."),
        ("0", "No action selected."),
        (str(10**30), "No action selected."),
    ],
)
def test_copying_needs_an_academy_to_copy_from(
    staff_client, tenants, copy_from, message
):
    Academy.objects.filter(pk=tenants.main.pk).update(features={"families": False})
    resp = staff_client.post(
        "/admin/tenants/academy/",
        {
            "action": "copy_features",
            "_selected_action": [tenants.other.pk],
            "copy_from": copy_from,
        },
        HTTP_HOST=PUBLIC_HOST,
        follow=True,
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert message in resp.content.decode()
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {}


def test_the_command_switches_features_by_subdomain(tenants, capsys):
    call_command("set_features", "pytest-other", "--off", "families", "--on", "levels")
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {"families": False, "levels": True}
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == "pytest-other: levels=on, families=off\n"
    call_command("set_features", "pytest-other", "--on", "families")
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {"families": True, "levels": True}
    assert stored(tenants.main) == {}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("args", "message"),
    [
        (["nowhere", "--on", "families"], "No academy has the subdomain 'nowhere'."),
        (["pytest-other"], "Name at least one feature with --on or --off."),
        (
            ["pytest-other", "--on", "families", "--off", "families"],
            "A feature can't be switched both on and off.",
        ),
        (["pytest-other", "--on", "olovix"], "Unknown feature: olovix."),
    ],
)
def test_the_command_refuses_what_it_cannot_do(tenants, args, message):
    with pytest.raises(CommandError, match=message):
        call_command("set_features", *args)
    assert stored(tenants.other) == {}
```

```python
# Plan 13 §5.5: the Academy change page's Features section.
ACADEMY_FIELDS = [
    "name",
    "subdomain",
    "schema_name",
    "status",
    "timezone",
    "currency",
    "plan_label",
    "plan_notes",
    "created_at",
]
GROUP_TITLES = {
    "people": "People",
    "teaching": "Teaching",
    "money": "Money",
    "communication": "Communication",
    "content": "Content",
    "platform": "Platform",
}
UNBUILT = "Takes effect when built."
FEATURES_NOTE = (
    "Only Etqan switches these; the academy's admin sees them read-only. "
    "Switching a feature off hides it and keeps its data."
)


def feature_field(code: str) -> str:
    return f"feature_{code}"


class _AcademyChangeForm(forms.ModelForm):
    """The academy's own fields and one checkbox per feature. Only the
    switches Etqan changes are stored; the others keep following their
    defaults."""

    class Meta:
        model = Academy
        fields = ["name", "status", "timezone", "currency", "plan_label", "plan_notes"]

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        stored = self.instance.features
        for feature in features.REGISTRY:
            field = self.fields[feature_field(feature.code)]
            field.initial = features.switched_on(feature.code, stored)
            held = features.held_by(feature.code, stored)
            if held:
                names = ", ".join(features.get(code).label_en for code in held)
                note = f"Held off: needs {names}."
                field.help_text = f"{field.help_text} {note}".strip()

    def save(self, commit=True):  # noqa: FBT002 -- Django's signature
        changed = {
            feature.code: self.cleaned_data[feature_field(feature.code)]
            for feature in features.REGISTRY
            if feature_field(feature.code) in self.changed_data
        }
        self.instance.features = {**self.instance.features, **changed}
        return super().save(commit=commit)


AcademyChangeForm = type(
    "AcademyChangeForm",
    (_AcademyChangeForm,),
    {
        feature_field(feature.code): forms.BooleanField(
            required=False,
            label=f"{feature.label_en} ({feature.label_ar})",
            help_text="" if feature.built else UNBUILT,
        )
        for feature in features.REGISTRY
    },
)


class AcademyActionForm(ActionForm):
    """The changelist's action bar, with the academy "Copy features from
    another academy" copies from."""

    copy_from = forms.ModelChoiceField(
        queryset=Academy.objects.exclude(schema_name=get_public_schema_name()),
        required=False,
        label="Copy features from",
    )


class DomainInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = Domain
    fields = ["domain", "status", "is_primary", "is_custom", "verified_at"]
```

Create `backend/etqan/tenants/management/commands/set_features.py`:

```python
"""Switch an academy's features from the command line (Plan 13 §5.5): the
platform admin's switches, for scripts and the e2e suite.

    manage.py set_features demo --off families --on supervision
"""

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.core.management.base import CommandError
from django.db import connection

from etqan.platform import features
from etqan.platform.exceptions import ValidationError
from etqan.tenants import services
from etqan.tenants.models import Academy


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Switch features on or off for one academy, found by its subdomain."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("subdomain")
        parser.add_argument("--on", nargs="+", default=[], metavar="CODE")
        parser.add_argument("--off", nargs="+", default=[], metavar="CODE")

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        on, off = options["on"], options["off"]
        if not on and not off:
            raise CommandError("Name at least one feature with --on or --off.")
        if set(on) & set(off):
            raise CommandError("A feature can't be switched both on and off.")
        connection.set_schema_to_public()
        subdomain = options["subdomain"]
        academy = Academy.objects.filter(subdomain=subdomain).first()
        if academy is None:
            raise CommandError(f"No academy has the subdomain {subdomain!r}.")
        values = {**dict.fromkeys(on, True), **dict.fromkeys(off, False)}
        try:
            services.set_features(academy, values)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            raise CommandError(exc.message) from exc
        now_on = features.on_codes(academy.features)
        states = ", ".join(
            f"{code}={'on' if code in now_on else 'off'}" for code in values
        )
        self.stdout.write(f"{academy.subdomain}: {states}")
```


with:

```python
    return academy


def set_features(academy: Academy, values: dict[str, bool]) -> Academy:
    """Etqan's switches for one academy (Plan 13 §5.5): ``values`` (code ->
    on) over what is stored; every other code keeps its stored value or its
    default. An unknown code is a 400 on ``features``, and nothing changes."""
    unknown = sorted(set(values) - features.CODES)
    if unknown:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"Unknown feature: {', '.join(unknown)}.", field="features"
        )
    academy.features = {
        **academy.features,
        **{code: bool(on) for code, on in values.items()},
    }
    Academy.objects.filter(pk=academy.pk).update(features=academy.features)
    return academy


def copy_features(source: Academy, targets) -> list[Academy]:
    """Plan 13 §5.5: give each of ``targets`` exactly ``source``'s stored
    switches; the source itself is left alone. Returns the academies it
    changed."""
    copied = []
    for academy in targets:
        if academy.pk == source.pk:
            continue
        academy.features = dict(source.features)
        Academy.objects.filter(pk=academy.pk).update(features=academy.features)
        copied.append(academy)
    return copied


def seed_features(academy: Academy, codes) -> list[str]:
    """Dev seeds (Plan 13 §7): switch ``codes`` on where nothing is stored
    for them yet, so a re-run never overrides a switch Etqan changed.
```

### Task 8: Dashboard: `hasFeature`, the nav, and "This feature isn't enabled"

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/src/features/shell/FeatureOff.tsx`
- Modify:
  - `dashboard/src/features/identity/schemas.ts`, `dashboard/src/features/identity/permissions.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/features/shell/nav.ts`, `dashboard/src/features/shell/AppShell.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/shell/PermissionGate.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/index.tsx`
  - the feature screens' route files under `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/`
  - `dashboard/src/locales/{en,ar}/common.json`
- Test: `dashboard/src/features/identity/permissions.test.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/shell/{AppShell,PermissionGate}.test.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/shell/nav.test.ts`, `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/index.test.tsx`, `dashboard/src/routes/permissions.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6's `me/.features`.
- Produces:
  - `FeatureCode` (the 8 built codes) and `Me.features?: readonly string[]`.
  - `hasFeature(me, code) -> boolean`: a missing list hides nothing.
  - `useHasFeature() -> (code: FeatureCode) => boolean`, provided by `CanProvider` for every role; nothing is hidden outside the shell.
  - `StaticDataRouteOption.feature?: FeatureCode` and `NavItem.feature?: FeatureCode`.
  - `visibleNavItems(items, profiles, role, allowed, hasFeature)`: the fifth argument is now a predicate, not a `Set`.
  - `FeatureOff` and the strings `features.off.{title,body}`.
- Breaks and updates:
  - `nav.ts`'s `Feature` type goes; `FeatureCode` replaces it.
  - 12b's two `AppShell` tests, which mocked the academy settings for the supervision item, now read `me.features`.
  - `nav.test.ts`'s supervision test passes predicates.

```ts
/** Plan 13 §6: the screens of a built feature, and the feature each names;
 * opened while it is off, they say it isn't enabled. A new screen inside
 * the app shell whose id names one of these features must join the list
 * (a printed page has no shell: its data's 404 speaks for it). */
const FEATURE_SCREENS: Record<string, string> = {
	"/_authed/people/families/": "families",
	"/_authed/people/families/$familyId": "families",
	"/_authed/people/parents/": "parents",
	"/_authed/people/parents/$personId": "parents",
	"/_authed/scheduling/reports": "session_reports",
	"/_authed/teaching/reports": "session_reports",
	"/_authed/billing/invoices/": "invoices",
	"/_authed/billing/invoices/$invoiceId": "invoices",
	"/_authed/billing/invoices/new": "invoices",
	"/_authed/learning/invoices/": "invoices",
	"/_authed/learning/invoices/$invoiceId": "invoices",
	"/_authed/scheduling/supervision": "supervision",
};
const FEATURE_WORDS = /families|parents|reports|invoices|supervision/;

describe("feature screens", () => {
	const all = Object.values(router.routesById) as AnyRoute[];

	it("names exactly the features in the list", () => {
		const named = Object.fromEntries(
			all
				.filter((route) => route.options.staticData?.feature)
				.map((route) => [route.id, route.options.staticData?.feature]),
		);
		expect(named).toEqual(FEATURE_SCREENS);
	});

	it("leaves no screen of a feature unnamed", () => {
		const unnamed = all.filter(
			(route) =>
				route.id.startsWith("/_authed/") &&
				FEATURE_WORDS.test(route.id) &&
				route.options.component &&
				!route.options.staticData?.feature,
		);
		expect(unnamed.map((route) => route.id)).toEqual([]);
	});
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/shell/FeatureOff.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ToggleLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { usePageTitle } from "@/features/branding";
import { Card, CardContent, EmptyState, PageContainer, PageHeader } from "@/ui";

/** Plan 13 §6: a screen of a feature the academy has switched off, opened
 * directly. Distinct from `NoAccess`: nobody here may use it, whatever
 * their roles. */
export function FeatureOff() {
	const { t } = useTranslation();
	usePageTitle(t("features.off.title"));
	return (
		<PageContainer>
			<PageHeader title={t("features.off.title")} />
			<Card>
				<CardContent>
					<EmptyState
						icon={ToggleLeft}
						title={t("features.off.body")}
						action={
							<Link
								to="/"
								className="font-medium text-primary-text underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
							>
								{t("access.noAccess.home")}
							</Link>
						}
					/>
				</CardContent>
			</Card>
		</PageContainer>
	);
}
```

```tsx
				.filter(Boolean)

	});
	if (needed && needed.split(" ").some((code) => !can(me, code))) {
```

with:

```tsx
				.filter(Boolean)
				.join(" "),
	});
	const features = useMatches({
		select: (matches) =>
			matches
				.map((match) => match.staticData.feature ?? "")
				.filter(Boolean)
				.join(" "),
	});
	if (needed && needed.split(" ").some((code) => !can(me, code))) {
		return <NoAccess />;
	}
	const off = features
		.split(" ")
		.filter(Boolean)
		.some((code) => !hasFeature(me, code as FeatureCode));
	if (off) return <FeatureOff />;
	return <>{children}</>;
}
```

```ts
];

/** `allowed` is `can` for the signed-in user (Plan 12a): an item that
 * names codes shows when any one of them is allowed. `features` are the
 * academy's switches that are on (Plan 12b). */
export function visibleNavItems(
	items: NavItem[],
	profiles: readonly ProfileType[],
	role?: Role,
	allowed: (code: string) => boolean = () => true,
	features: ReadonlySet<Feature> = new Set(),
): NavItem[] {
	return items.filter((i) => {
		if (i.feature && !features.has(i.feature)) return false;
		if (i.requiresRole) {
			const roles: readonly Role[] =
				typeof i.requiresRole === "string" ? [i.requiresRole] : i.requiresRole;
```

with:

```ts
];

/** `allowed` is `can` for the signed-in user (Plan 12a): an item that
 * names codes shows when any one of them is allowed. `hasFeature` is
 * whether the academy has a feature on (Plan 13): an item that names one
 * shows only while it is. */
export function visibleNavItems(
	items: NavItem[],
	profiles: readonly ProfileType[],
	role?: Role,
	allowed: (code: string) => boolean = () => true,
	hasFeature: (code: FeatureCode) => boolean = () => true,
): NavItem[] {
	return items.filter((i) => {
		if (i.feature && !hasFeature(i.feature)) return false;
		if (i.requiresRole) {
			const roles: readonly Role[] =
				typeof i.requiresRole === "string" ? [i.requiresRole] : i.requiresRole;
```

### Task 9: Dashboard: buttons and sections of a switched-off feature

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/src/components/ExportButton.tsx`
- Modify:
  - `dashboard/src/features/people/{PeopleList,AdminsList,StudentsList,StudentForm}.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/features/catalogue/CatalogueList.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/features/scheduling/{SubscriptionsList,SessionsList,SubscriptionDetail,SessionPage,TeacherSessionTable}.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/features/payroll/PayslipsList.tsx`, `dashboard/src/features/billing/InvoicesList.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/test/access-fixtures.ts`
- Test:
  - new: `dashboard/src/components/ExportButton.test.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/features/people/{StudentsList,StudentForm}.test.tsx`
  - `dashboard/src/features/scheduling/{SubscriptionsList,SubscriptionDetail,SessionPage,TeacherSessions}.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8's `useHasFeature` and `CanProvider`.
- Produces:
  - `ExportButton({ href })`: the "Export CSV" link, only while `export` is on.
  - `adminWith(...features: FeatureCode[]): Me`, a test fixture.
  - With a feature off:
    - `families`: no Account type column, family filters or bulk family actions on the students list, and no family facts on the student page;
    - `parents`: no Guardians panel on the student page;
    - `invoices`: no Invoices panel on the subscription page, and no Payment column on the subscriptions list;
    - `session_reports`: no Report card on the session page, and no Report column in a teacher's table;
    - `teacher_attendance`: no Attendance column in a teacher's table.
- The existing tests keep passing unchanged: outside the shell, and with a `Me` that lists no features, nothing is hidden.

```tsx
	});
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `dashboard/src/components/ExportButton.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useHasFeature } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { Button } from "@/ui";

/** A list's "Export CSV" link (`?format=csv`), shown only while the academy
 * has export switched on (Plan 13 §5.3: with it off the server answers 404). */
export function ExportButton({ href }: { href: string }) {
	const { t } = useTranslation();
	const hasFeature = useHasFeature();
	if (!hasFeature("export")) return null;
	return (
		<Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
			<a href={href} download>
				{t("people.exportCsv")}
			</a>
		</Button>
	);
}
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/academy/FeaturesPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { act } from "react";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "@/lib/i18n";
import i18n from "@/lib/i18n";
import { type AcademyFeature, academyApi } from "./api";
import { FeaturesPage } from "./FeaturesPage";

vi.mock("./api", async (orig) => {
	const actual = await orig<typeof import("./api")>();
	return { ...actual, academyApi: { ...actual.academyApi, features: vi.fn() } };
});

const feature = (
	fields: Partial<AcademyFeature> & Pick<AcademyFeature, "code">,
): AcademyFeature => ({
	label_ar: `ميزة ${fields.code}`,
	label_en: `Feature ${fields.code}`,
	group: "people",
	on: true,
	built: true,
	held_by: [],
	...fields,
});

const FEATURES = [
	feature({
		code: "families",
		label_en: "Family accounts",
		label_ar: "نظام حسابات العائلات",
	}),
	feature({
		code: "parents",
		label_en: "Parents and guardians",
		on: false,
	}),
	feature({
		code: "session_reports",
		label_en: "Session reports",
		group: "teaching",
		on: false,
	}),
	feature({
		code: "report_deductions",
		label_en: "Report deductions",
		label_ar: "خصومات التقارير",
		group: "money",
		on: false,
		built: false,
		held_by: ["session_reports"],
	}),
];

function renderPage() {
	render(
		<QueryClientProvider
			client={
				new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })
			}
		>
			<FeaturesPage />
		</QueryClientProvider>,
	);
}

describe("FeaturesPage", () => {
	beforeEach(() => {
		vi.clearAllMocks();
		vi.mocked(academyApi.features).mockResolvedValue(FEATURES);
	});

	it("lists every feature by group, read-only, as managed by Etqan", async () => {
		renderPage();
		const people = await screen.findByRole("list", { name: "People" });
		expect(
			within(people).getByRole("listitem", { name: "Family accounts" }),
		).toHaveTextContent("On");
		expect(
			within(people).getByRole("listitem", { name: "Parents and guardians" }),
		).toHaveTextContent("Off");
		const money = screen.getByRole("list", { name: "Money" });
		const held = within(money).getByRole("listitem", {
			name: "Report deductions",
		});
		expect(held).toHaveTextContent("Takes effect when built");
		expect(held).toHaveTextContent("Held off: needs Session reports");
		// Only the groups that have features, in the registry's order.
		expect(
			screen
				.getAllByRole("list")
				.map((list) => list.getAttribute("aria-label")),
		).toEqual(["People", "Teaching", "Money"]);
		expect(screen.getByText("Managed by Etqan")).toBeInTheDocument();
		expect(screen.queryByRole("checkbox")).toBeNull();
		expect(screen.queryByRole("button")).toBeNull();
	});

	it("names the features in Arabic", async () => {
		await act(() => i18n.changeLanguage("ar"));
		renderPage();
		const people = await screen.findByRole("list", { name: "الأشخاص" });
		expect(
			within(people).getByRole("listitem", { name: "نظام حسابات العائلات" }),
		).toHaveTextContent("مفعّلة");
		expect(screen.getByText("تديرها إتقان")).toBeInTheDocument();
		await act(() => i18n.changeLanguage("en"));
	});

	it("says so when the features could not be loaded", async () => {
		vi.mocked(academyApi.features).mockRejectedValue(new Error("down"));
		renderPage();
		expect(
			await screen.findByText("The features could not be loaded."),
		).toBeInTheDocument();
	});
});
```

Create `dashboard/src/features/academy/FeaturesPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import {
	Alert,
	AlertDescription,
	Card,
	CardContent,
	CardHeader,
	CardTitle,
	Spinner,
	StatusChip,
} from "@/ui";
import { type AcademyFeature, FEATURE_GROUPS } from "./api";
import { useAcademyFeatures } from "./queries";

/**
 * Plan 13 §5.6, §6: Settings → Features. Every feature, grouped, and
 * whether it is on for this academy. Read-only: Etqan switches them.
 */
export function FeaturesPage() {
	const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
	const { data, isPending, isError } = useAcademyFeatures();
	if (isError) {
		return (
			<Alert variant="destructive">
				<AlertDescription>{t("features.loadError")}</AlertDescription>
			</Alert>
		);
	}
	if (isPending) return <Spinner />;
	const name = (feature: AcademyFeature) =>
		i18n.language === "ar" ? feature.label_ar : feature.label_en;
	const byCode = new Map(data.map((feature) => [feature.code, feature]));
	const names = (codes: string[]) =>
		codes
			.map((code) => byCode.get(code))
			.filter((feature) => feature !== undefined)
			.map(name)
			.join(", ");
	return (
		<div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
			<div>
				<StatusChip>{t("features.managed")}</StatusChip>
			</div>
			{FEATURE_GROUPS.map((group) => {
				const rows = data.filter((feature) => feature.group === group);
				if (rows.length === 0) return null;
				const title = t(`features.groups.${group}`);
				return (
					<Card key={group}>
						<CardHeader className="border-b border-border">
							<CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>
						</CardHeader>
						<CardContent>
							<ul aria-label={title} className="flex flex-col">
								{rows.map((feature) => (
									<li
										key={feature.code}
										aria-label={name(feature)}
										className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-border py-3 last:border-b-0"
									>
										<div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1">
											<span className="font-medium">{name(feature)}</span>
											{feature.built ? null : (
												<span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
													{t("features.unbuilt")}
												</span>
											)}
											{feature.held_by.length > 0 ? (
												<span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
													{t("features.heldBy", {
														names: names(feature.held_by),
													})}
												</span>
											) : null}
										</div>
										<StatusChip tone={feature.on ? "live" : "neutral"}>
											{feature.on
												? t("features.state.on")
												: t("features.state.off")}
										</StatusChip>
									</li>
								))}
							</ul>
						</CardContent>
					</Card>
				);
			})}
		</div>
	);
}
```

```ts
export const FEATURE_GROUPS = [
	"people",
	"teaching",
	"money",
	"communication",
	"content",
	"platform",
] as const;
export type FeatureGroup = (typeof FEATURE_GROUPS)[number];

/** Plan 13 §5.6: one of the academy's features, as Etqan has set it. */
export interface AcademyFeature {
	code: string;
	label_ar: string;
	label_en: string;
	group: FeatureGroup;
	on: boolean;
	/** Whether any part of the product checks it yet. */
	built: boolean;
	/** The prerequisites holding a switched-on feature off. */
	held_by: string[];
}

export const academyApi = {
	get: async () => (await api.get<AcademySettings>("academy/settings/")).data,
	update: async (body: Partial<Omit<AcademySettings, "supervision_enabled">>) =>
		(await api.patch<AcademySettings>("academy/settings/", body)).data,
	features: async () =>
		(await api.get<AcademyFeature[]>("academy/features/")).data,
};
```

Create `dashboard/src/routes/_authed/settings.features.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { FeaturesPage } from "@/features/academy";
import { usePageTitle } from "@/features/branding";
import { ADMINS_ONLY } from "@/features/identity/permissions";
import { requireOffice } from "@/features/identity/require-admin";
import { PageContainer, PageHeader } from "@/ui";

/** Plan 13 §5.6: admins only, as the route behind it (no staff code). */
export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authed/settings/features")({
	staticData: { permission: ADMINS_ONLY },
	beforeLoad: ({ context }) => requireOffice(context),
	component: function FeaturesRoute() {
		const { t } = useTranslation();
		usePageTitle(t("nav.features"));
		return (
			<PageContainer>
				<PageHeader
					title={t("nav.features")}
					description={t("features.subtitle")}
				/>
				<FeaturesPage />
			</PageContainer>
		);
	},
});
```

### Task 11: End-to-end through Caddy: Etqan switches families off and on for demo; STATE.md

**Files:**
- Create: `dashboard/e2e/features.spec.ts`
- Modify: `dashboard/e2e/supervision.spec.ts`, `STATE.md` (meta), submodule pointers (meta, after merge)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 1–10;
  - `e2e/fixtures.ts`'s `login`, `expectLoggedIn`, `DEMO_URL` and `DEMO_ADMIN`;
  - `e2e/manage.ts`'s `manage()`, which runs `manage.py` locally through `E2E_PYTHON` / `E2E_BACKEND_DIR`, or on a deployed server through `E2E_MANAGE`;
  - Task 7's `set_features` command;
  - the seeded "Omar family".
- Produces: `e2e/features.spec.ts` (spec §8's journey).
- CI: no change. The `e2e` job already migrates and runs `seed_dev` (which now switches every built feature on for `demo`). `manage()` finds `manage.py` beside the dashboard in the meta checkout.
- Breaks and updates: `supervision.spec.ts` toggled the General supervision checkbox, which is now read-only. It makes sure the feature is on with `set_features` and checks the checkbox is checked and disabled.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the journey and update the supervision journey**

The spec switches families back on in `finally`: the suite shares one seeded database, and a second run must see demo as seeded.
- The family is found through the list's search, since earlier runs add families of their own.
- The dashboard reads the features with `me/`, so the spec reloads `/app/` after each switch.

Create `dashboard/e2e/features.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { DEMO_ADMIN, DEMO_URL, expectLoggedIn, login } from "./fixtures";
import { manage } from "./manage";

const OFF = "This feature isn't enabled for your academy.";

/** The seeded demo family, found through the list's search: a database
 * that ran the suite before holds more families than one page. */
async function omarFamily(page: Page) {
	await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/people/families`);
	await page.getByRole("searchbox").fill("Omar family");
	return page.getByRole("row", { name: /Omar family/ });
}

// Plan 13 spec §8: Etqan switches family accounts off for demo (with
// `set_features`, the platform admin's command line); the academy's admin
// no longer sees Families, a direct link says the feature isn't enabled,
// and Settings → Features shows it off; switched back on, the family is
// there as before. It is switched back on whatever happens, so the rest of
// the suite, and a second run, see demo as seeded.
test("Etqan switches family accounts off for demo, and on again", async ({
	page,
}) => {
	manage("set_features", "demo", "--on", "families");
	await login(page, DEMO_URL, DEMO_ADMIN);
	await expectLoggedIn(page, /demo academy admin/i);
	await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem("etqan-locale", "en"));
	await expect(await omarFamily(page)).toContainText("Yusuf Omar");

	try {
		expect(manage("set_features", "demo", "--off", "families")).toContain(
			"demo: families=off",
		);
		// A fresh load reads me/ again, with the academy's features.
		await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/`);
		const nav = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main navigation" });
		await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name: "Students" })).toBeVisible();
		await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name: "Families" })).toHaveCount(0);

		await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/people/families`);
		await expect(
			page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: OFF }),
		).toBeVisible();

		await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/settings/features`);
		const people = page.getByRole("list", { name: "People" });
		await expect(
			people.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Family accounts" }),
		).toContainText("Off");
		await expect(page.getByText("Managed by Etqan")).toBeVisible();
	} finally {
		manage("set_features", "demo", "--on", "families");
	}

	await page.goto(`${DEMO_URL}/app/`);
	await expect(
		page
			.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main navigation" })
			.getByRole("link", { name: "Families" }),
	).toBeVisible();
	await expect(await omarFamily(page)).toContainText("Yusuf Omar");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the suite through Caddy, as CI does**

- **Backend.** Serve a freshly migrated and seeded database: `migrate_schemas --shared`, then `seed_dev`, with `config.settings.local`, the file email backend and eager Celery (the CI job's environment). Then `runserver 127.0.0.1:8000`.
- **Dashboard.** `pnpm build` and `pnpm preview --port 4173 --host 127.0.0.1`.
- **Marketing.** Build it and serve `node dist/server/entry.mjs` on `:4321`.
- **The edge.** A `caddy:2.11.4-alpine` container on the host network with `caddy/Caddyfile.e2e`.

Then, from `dashboard/`:

```bash
E2E_DEMO_URL=http://demo.etqan.localhost E2E_OTHER_URL=http://other.etqan.localhost \
E2E_MAIL_DIR=/tmp/etqan-mail E2E_PYTHON="$PWD/../backend/.venv/bin/python" CI=1 \
npx pnpm@10 exec playwright test --retries=0 --workers=1
```

The Playwright process needs the backend's environment too (`DATABASE_URL`, `DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE`, the mail settings): `manage()` runs `manage.py` with this process's environment.

Expected: 23 passed, `features.spec.ts` and `supervision.spec.ts` included. Run it a second time on the same database: 23 passed again.

In `STATE.md`, replace:

```markdown
## Where we are

Plan 12b (session supervision, B1 plan 3 of 4, second half) built and in review: branch
`feat/supervision` in backend, dashboard and meta (spec
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-roles-permissions-design.md`, plan
`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-29-plan-12b-supervision.md`). Plan 12a (staff roles and permissions)
is merged. `AcademySettings.supervision_enabled` ("General supervision", off by default) gates it all:
while off, no supervisor field is sent, the supervision routes and the session PATCH answer 404, and a
`supervisor_id` in a subscription body is ignored; the stored values are kept. A supervisor is an
active staff account holding `session.supervise` through an active role
(`access.services.supervisors_queryset` / `get_supervisor`). `Subscription.supervisor` and
```

with:

```markdown

## Where we are

Plan 13 (per-academy feature toggles, B1 plan 4 of 4) built and in review: branch `feat/features`
in backend, dashboard and meta (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-30-feature-toggles-design.md`,
plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-30-plan-13-feature-toggles.md`). `etqan.platform.features` is
the registry (36 features: 8 built, 28 stored for later phases, off by default); the values are
`Academy.features` (public schema, code → boolean, `db_default={}`, a missing code means the
default). `features.enabled(code)` reads `connection.tenant` (no query; the platform never imports
tenants). A route declares `feature` next to `permission_codes` and lists `FeatureOn` after the
code check (404 while off); the route table's `FEATURES` column and its guard words keep them
declared. Off, `families` hides the family fields, filters, CSV columns and bulk actions and the
family payer; `parents` refuses parent sign-in (a signed-in parent is signed out), drops `me/`'s
children, guardian payers and guardian notices; `invoices` stops automatic invoices, invoice notices
and the subscriptions' payment status; `session_reports` stops `report.missing`;
`auto_notifications` skips the academy's scan; `teacher_attendance` refuses teachers' marks (403);

keeps the defaults. The e2e suite switches families off and on for demo.
```

## Self-review

Checked against the spec after writing; fixes were made inline.

**Spec coverage**

| Spec | Task |
|---|---|
| FT-1: Etqan sets, the admin sees | 7 (platform admin, command), 6 (`academy/features/`), 10 (read-only page) |
| FT-2: all 36 switches live now; the unbuilt ones take effect when built | 1 (registry), 7 ("Takes effect when built."), 10 |
| FT-3: a registry in code, values on the academy | 1 |
| FT-4: off hides, never deletes | 3, 4, 5, 11 (each test switches back on and finds the same data) |
| FT-5: the business rules stay settings; General supervision moves | 2 |
| §3.1–§3.2: codes, labels, groups, defaults, `built`, `requires` | 1 (D1's table) |
| §3.3: the effective value; unknown codes ignored; the admin shows held-off features | 1, 7 |
| §4: `Academy.features`, `db_default {}`; the supervision data migration; no tenant change | 1, 2 |
| §5.1: `enabled(code)` from `connection.tenant`, no query; background jobs inside `for_each_academy`; unknown code raises | 1, 5 |
| §5.2: `feature` (or a per-method map); the check after `HasCode`, 404; the route table's feature column and its failing test | 3 |
| §5.3: each built feature's effects | 3 (routes, bulk family actions), 4 (payloads, payer rule, sign-in, `me/` children, invoices, teacher attendance, export), 5 (scanner), 2 (supervision) |
| §5.4: `me/.features` | 6 |
| §5.5: Features section, grouped, prerequisites, copy action, history; no tenant API writes | 7, 6 (`test_nothing_in_the_academys_api_writes_them`) |
| §5.6: `GET academy/features/`, admins only; Settings → Features "Managed by Etqan"; the General supervision switch read-only | 6, 10 |
| §6: `hasFeature`; nav, `staticData`, buttons; "This feature isn't enabled for your academy."; en/ar | 8, 9, 10 |
| §7: demo all built on; other defaults; idempotent, never overriding Etqan | 2 (D8) |
| §8: registry, effective value, routes, each feature off, restore, migration, isolation, platform admin, dashboard, e2e | 1–11 |
| §9 risks: route left reachable (route-table test), job ignoring a switch (scanner tests), data lost (restore tests), deploy changing an academy (defaults + migration), unbuilt switch misread (the "takes effect when built" marks) | 3, 5, 4, 2, 7, 10 |

**Spec gaps settled here:**
- the registry's exact codes, Arabic spellings and order (D1);
- where the supervision migration runs, and academies that lack the column (D2);
- the feature column's shape, and guard words for routes not yet listed (D3);
- a parent already signed in, guardians' notices, payment status and the parent family payer (D4);
- `held_by` semantics (D1, D5);
- storing only what Etqan changed, and a junk copy source (D6);
- a missing `me/.features`, and the pages behind a parent-only sign-in (D7);
- seeds folded into Task 2 (D8, D10);
- the e2e using the platform admin's command line (D9).

**Placeholder scan:** no "TBD", "TODO", "similar to Task N" or code step without its code. Every name a later task uses is produced by an earlier task's Interfaces block.

**Type and name consistency:**
- **Backend names.** The helpers from Task 1 are used by Tasks 2–7: `features.enabled`, `enabled_codes`, `describe`, `BUILT`, `CODES`, `get`, `switched_on`, `held_by` and `on_codes`.
  - `FEATURE_OFF` and `FeatureOn` (Task 3) are what Task 4's bulk-family 404 and the route-table tests use.
  - `set_features` is both the root fixture (Task 1) and `tenants.services.set_features` (Task 7). They are different objects in different places, and neither imports the other.
- **The payloads match the dashboard.** The `me/.features` codes (Task 6) are `FeatureCode` (Task 8).
  - The `academy/features/` fields (Task 6) are `AcademyFeature` (Task 10).
  - The group names are `features.GROUPS` in the backend and `FEATURE_GROUPS` in the dashboard.
- **Strings and commands.** The 404 message and the two 403 messages are the same strings in the backend and its tests. The dashboard shows its own translated texts.
  - `set_features` (Task 7) prints `demo: families=off`, which Task 11 asserts.

**Review Focus:** each of the five lines names its tests, which are in the owning task's test files.

**Execution:** Plan complete. Please review the plan and choose an execution method: subagent-driven (a fresh implementer and reviewer per task) or native.

I recommend **subagent-driven**, for three reasons:
- the eleven tasks lean on each other's names (the registry helpers, `FeatureOn`, `me/.features`);
- the backend tasks each touch several apps;
- a shipped mistake here hides or exposes whole parts of every academy.
